11. Core Schema and Core Types

Normative API surface. Phases 0–13 (## 12–## 25) build against these EXACT signatures — no renaming, no drift. Grouped by crate; signatures + doc-comments only (bodies ≤2 illustrative lines). Distance convention everywhere: smaller = closer, nearest-first; InnerProduct/Cosine stored as negated-similarity so every heap orders uniformly.

Cross-cutting property legend used per type: Own (ownership/lifetime) · Mut (mutation semantics) · Conc (Send+Sync vs single-writer) · Persist (serde/mmap) · Compat (stability).

Width contract (LOCKED across crates):

Type Width Cap Sentinel Rationale
VectorId u32 < 2^31 u32::MAX = INVALID 4 B/ref keeps CSR adjacency cache-dense
Distance f32 finite (NaN rejected) +inf = unreachable SIMD-native; total order via f32::total_cmp
degree / m / R u32 R ≤ 512 — bounds NeighborList allocation + dist-comps/hop
ef / ef_construction / L u32 ≤ 4096 — bounds frontier + VisitedSet working set

11.1 ann-core — vectors, metric, distance, kernels

/// Dense-vector handle; index of a row in VectorStore. Stable across save/mmap.
/// Own: Copy POD. Mut: value. Conc: Send+Sync+Copy. Persist: u32 LE. Compat: width FROZEN at u32.
pub struct VectorId(pub u32);

/// Metric selector. Cosine assumes L2-normalized vectors (normalized at VectorStore::push).
/// Chosen at build, written to header, checked on open (search metric MUST equal build metric).
/// Own: Copy. Conc: Send+Sync. Persist: 1-byte tag {0=L2,1=InnerProduct,2=Cosine}.
pub enum Metric { L2, InnerProduct, Cosine }

/// Scalar distance; SMALLER = CLOSER. InnerProduct/Cosine = negated similarity so one min-heap
/// orders all metrics. Own: Copy. Ord: f32::total_cmp (finite). Invariant: never NaN (rejected upstream).
pub struct Distance(pub f32);

/// Owning, row-major, contiguous, 32-byte-aligned f32 store. data.len() == dim*len.
/// Own: owns Vec<f32>; the single source of coordinates. Mut: append-only via push() during build,
/// immutable during search. Conc: &VectorStore Send+Sync after freeze. Persist: header{dim,len} +
/// aligned f32 payload mmap'd zero-copy. Compat: layout FROZEN.
pub struct VectorStore { dim: usize, data: Vec<f32>, len: u32 }
impl VectorStore {
    pub fn get(&self, id: VectorId) -> &[f32];     // &data[id*dim .. id*dim+dim], no copy
    pub fn push(&mut self, v: &[f32]) -> VectorId; // normalizes if Cosine; build phase only
    pub fn dim(&self) -> usize; pub fn len(&self) -> u32;
}

/// SIMD-or-scalar distance fn, selected once at startup via runtime CPU feature dispatch (Phase 1).
/// Pure, alloc-free, stateless. Differential gate: |simd - scalar| <= 1e-5 rel error.
/// Conc: Send+Sync. Kernels: scalar + AVX2/AVX-512/NEON L2/InnerProduct/Cosine.
pub trait DistanceKernel { fn distance(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> Distance; }

/// Distance-computations budget tracker; one inc per kernel call. AtomicU64 -> Sync.
/// Primary cost metric for CostModel + Autotuner (not wall-clock alone).
pub struct DistanceCounter { count: AtomicU64 }

Exact-KNN baseline (Phase 0, brute force, O(N·dim)) is the ground-truth source feeding RecallHarness — NOT a query path.

11.2 ann-graph — adjacency, layers, graph forms

/// One node's out-edges: ids + parallel dists, sorted ASC by dist, capped at R.
/// WHY sorted: prune/heuristic scan stops early; neighbors[0] is closest. WHY capped: bounds
/// out-degree -> bounds dist-comps/hop -> bounds query cost. SmallVec inlines common small degree.
/// Own: owns ids/dists. Mut: &mut during build/prune; len <= R. Conc: per-node-locked on concurrent
/// insert (Phase 10); read-shared once frozen.
pub struct NeighborList { ids: SmallVec<[VectorId; R]>, dists: SmallVec<[Distance; R]> }

/// Mutable build form: one NeighborList per VectorId (index = id). Resized during insert/optimize.
pub struct AdjacencyList { neighbors: Vec<NeighborList> }

/// One HNSW layer (level 0 = base, all nodes). Vamana uses a single Layer{level:0}.
pub struct Layer { adjacency: AdjacencyList, level: u8 }

/// MUTABLE graph: the build/optimize working form. Mutated by HnswBuilder/VamanaBuilder and ALL
/// ann-optimize passes. Own: owns layers. Mut: single-writer &mut. Conc: NOT Sync for writes.
/// Persist: header{kind,metric,max_degree,entry_point} + per-layer adjacency.
pub struct Graph {
    layers: Vec<Layer>,   // layers[0] base; HNSW only is multi-layer
    entry_point: VectorId,
    max_degree: u32,      // R / m_max0 — the degree-invariant ceiling
    metric: Metric,
    kind: GraphKind,
}

/// Per-family build constants carried inside the graph. ml = 1/ln(m).
pub enum GraphKind {
    Hnsw   { m: u32, m_max0: u32, ef_construction: u32, ml: f32 },
    Vamana { r: u32, alpha: f32, l: u32 },
}

/// IMMUTABLE optimized form: CSR adjacency. edges[offsets[i]..offsets[i+1]] = out-neighbors of i
/// (sorted). offsets.len()==N+1. Cache-dense contiguous scan; THE search-time structure.
/// Own: owns both Vecs. Mut: NONE after freeze (rebuild to change topology). Conc: Send+Sync (read-only)
/// — the concurrent-search substrate. Persist: offsets:Vec<u32> + edges:Vec<VectorId> mmap'd zero-copy.
/// Compat: CSR layout FROZEN.
pub struct CsrGraph { offsets: Vec<u32>, edges: Vec<VectorId>, entry_point: VectorId }
impl CsrGraph {
    pub fn from_graph(g: &Graph) -> CsrGraph;        // freeze: flatten base layer to CSR
    pub fn neighbors(&self, id: VectorId) -> &[VectorId];
}

Mutable vs frozen. Build/optimize mutate Graph (per-node NeighborList); sealing compacts it into CsrGraph. CsrGraph is the only form search touches → read-only, race-free, cache-dense traversal. entry_point = single base-layer start, reset by reselect_entry_point.

11.3 ann-build — params + neighbor selection

/// All build knobs; one seed for deterministic level assignment (differential testing).
/// Own: Copy. Conc: Send+Sync. Persist: recorded in graph header for provenance.
pub struct BuildParams { m: u32, m_max0: u32, ef_construction: u32, alpha: f32, r: u32, l: u32, seed: u64 }

/// HNSW Algorithm 4. candidates sorted ASC by dist to q. Keep e iff d(e,q) < d(e,r) for every already
/// selected r (e closer to q than to any kept neighbor) -> diverse, navigable set vs naive nearest-M.
/// extend = consider neighbors-of-candidates; keep_pruned = backfill to m. Returns capped sorted list.
pub fn select_neighbors_heuristic(
    candidates: &[(VectorId, Distance)], m: u32, extend: bool, keep_pruned: bool,
) -> NeighborList;

/// Vamana RobustPrune. candidates = visited pool from greedy search. Loop: pop closest p*; edge p->p*;
/// drop every v with alpha*d(p*,v) <= d(p,v) (occlusion, relaxation alpha>=1); stop at R or empty.
pub fn robust_prune(
    p: VectorId, candidates: &mut Vec<(VectorId, Distance)>, alpha: f32, r: u32, store: &VectorStore,
) -> NeighborList;

HnswBuilder / VamanaBuilder consume &VectorStore + BuildParams → produce Graph. Level assignment L ~ floor(-ln(U)*ml) seeded by seed. Medoid computation (Vamana entry) lives here.

11.4 ann-search — search state, params, execution, recall

pub struct SearchParams { k: u32, ef: u32, beam_width: u32 } // ef>=k; beam_width=1 = pure greedy

/// Versioned bitset: O(1) reset between queries (bump epoch, no zeroing). Per-query mutable.
pub struct VisitedSet { /* Vec<u32> epoch stamps + current epoch */ }

/// Per-query scratch. REUSED across queries on a thread -> zero-heap-alloc steady state (clear+reuse).
/// candidates = min-heap (Reverse) frontier (expand closest first); results = bounded max-heap of ef/k.
/// Own: owns its three structures. Mut: &mut per query. Conc: NOT Sync — one per thread/searcher.
pub struct SearchContext {
    visited: VisitedSet,
    candidates: BinaryHeap<Reverse<(Distance, VectorId)>>,
    results: BinaryHeap<(Distance, VectorId)>,
}
pub struct SearchResult { pub id: VectorId, pub distance: Distance }

/// Layer-aware greedy over mutable Graph (build-time + HNSW search). Working list size ef, seeded at ep.
pub fn greedy_search(g: &Graph, q: &[f32], ep: VectorId, ef: u32, ctx: &mut SearchContext) -> Vec<SearchResult>;

/// Production search over frozen CsrGraph. Expand closest unexpanded; stop when closest unexpanded >
/// worst-in-W; return top-k. &CsrGraph Sync => N threads, N SearchContexts, zero contention.
pub fn beam_search(g: &CsrGraph, q: &[f32], params: &SearchParams, ctx: &mut SearchContext) -> Vec<SearchResult>;

/// Exact-KNN ground truth for recall@k. ground_truth[i] = true top-k of query i (brute force).
/// Own: owns the truth set. Conc: Send+Sync (read). Used only by recall/tune/bench, never a query path.
pub struct RecallHarness { ground_truth: Vec<Vec<VectorId>> }
impl RecallHarness { pub fn recall_at_k(&self, results: &[Vec<SearchResult>], k: u32) -> f32; }

Concurrency contract: one SearchContext per thread per query (mutable, non-Sync); &CsrGraph + &VectorStore shared Sync. The only safe concurrent search path. Filtered + batch search (Phase 5) reuse the same SearchContext.

11.5 ann-optimize — diagnostics, pruning, repair, cost, autotune (project center)

/// Full graph-quality report (Phase 7). Every field normative:
pub struct GraphDiagnostics {
    mean_out_degree: f32,         // avg |out(v)|
    out_degree_hist: [u32; 64],   // bucketed out-degree distribution
    min_in_degree: u32,           // lowest reverse-edge count (orphan risk)
    orphan_count: u32,            // nodes with in-degree == 0
    connected_components: u32,    // weak components; healthy graph == 1
    unreachable_from_ep: u32,     // nodes not reachable from entry_point via directed edges
    navigability_score: f32,      // sampled expected-hops-to-convergence proxy (lower = better)
    estimated_recall_at_10: f32,  // sampled queries vs RecallHarness exact KNN at fixed ef
}

/// Edge-selection strategy for re-pruning. RobustPrune = alpha-occlusion (keeps long-range edges);
/// HeuristicSelect = HNSW occlusion rule (keep_pruned backfill); RelativeNeighborhood = RNG (alpha=1 limit).
/// Own: Copy. Conc: Send+Sync.
pub enum PruneStrategy { RobustPrune { alpha: f32 }, HeuristicSelect { keep_pruned: bool }, RelativeNeighborhood }

pub fn occlusion_prune(g: &mut Graph, strat: PruneStrategy, store: &VectorStore); // re-prune for navigability
pub fn degree_rebalance(g: &mut Graph, r: u32);                  // enforce out-degree <= r, drop weakest
pub fn reverse_edge_repair(g: &mut Graph, min_in_degree: u32);   // add reverse edges to orphan/low-in-degree
pub fn connect_components(g: &mut Graph, store: &VectorStore);    // splice unreachable subgraphs into EP component
pub fn reselect_entry_point(g: &Graph, store: &VectorStore) -> VectorId; // medoid/centrality choice

/// Predicts cost without running: mean dist-comps/query ~ ef * mean_out_degree * (1 + hop_factor).
pub struct CostModel { /* fitted hop_factor + degree term */ }
pub struct RecallTarget { pub k: u32, pub recall: f32 }          // e.g. recall@10 >= 0.95

/// Bisection over ef to the smallest ef meeting RecallTarget, then grid over (alpha,R,L).
/// Returns the recall/cost Pareto frontier. Conc: trials run in parallel over the immutable index.
pub struct Autotuner { /* search state */ }
pub struct TuneResult { pub params: SearchParams, pub recall: f32, pub mean_dist_comps: f32, pub p99_us: f32 }

OPTIMIZER GUARANTEE (invariant, test-enforced): each pass may only improve or preserve estimated_recall_at_k at fixed ef on the diagnostic sample (no silent regression); out-degree never exceeds R; after connect_components → unreachable_from_ep == 0 and connected_components == 1. All passes take &mut Graph (single-writer); diagnostics + reselect_entry_point take &Graph.

11.6 ann-store — persistence + compression

/// Trained PQ: dim split into m_subspaces subspaces, ksub centroids each (ksub=256 -> 1 byte/code).
/// codebooks = flat [m_subspaces*ksub*sub_dim] f32 arena. Asymmetric distance via per-query LUT (no decode).
/// Own: owns codebooks. Mut: immutable after train. Conc: &ProductQuantizer Send+Sync (shared read).
/// Persist: header{m_subspaces,ksub} + codebook payload mmap-friendly. Compat: layout FROZEN.
pub struct ProductQuantizer { m_subspaces: u32, ksub: u32, codebooks: Vec<f32> }

pub struct Tombstone { pub id: VectorId }  // logical delete marker
/// Packed deleted-id set; filtered out of results pre-rerank. Own: owns bits. Conc: read-shared by
/// searchers, flipped by the writer. Persist: bitfield over n nodes.
pub struct DeleteSet { bits: BitVec }

/// Serialize frozen CsrGraph + vectors. Header: magic, version, metric, dim, kind, R, crc32.
/// Atomic: write temp + fsync + rename. Compat: version-mismatch / metric / dim mismatch => Err.
pub fn save_graph(g: &CsrGraph, store: &VectorStore, path: &Path) -> io::Result<()>;
/// Zero-copy mmap load; MappedGraph derefs to a CsrGraph view. Verifies magic+version+crc first.
pub fn mmap_graph(path: &Path) -> io::Result<MappedGraph>;

Search integration. DiskANN-style beam-search ranks PQ-coded candidates by asymmetric distance, then re-ranks with full-precision DistanceKernel before returning top-k (recall@10 target preserved). DeleteSet applied at query time (skip tombstoned ids); consolidation (Phase 10) rewrites CSR to physically drop tombstones. Endianness little-endian throughout; FlatStorage rows + CSR arenas 32-byte aligned for direct SIMD load off the map.

11.7 Cross-cutting type guarantees

Concern Guarantee
Send+Sync (shared-immutable) VectorStore, CsrGraph, ProductQuantizer, DistanceCounter, RecallHarness (frozen forms)
Thread-local / single-writer SearchContext, VisitedSet; Graph during build/optimize; all ann-optimize passes
Zero-alloc hot path SearchContext reused per query (epoch-reset VisitedSet, no realloc); CsrGraph + codebooks mmap'd, never copied
ID width VectorId pinned u32 (< 2^31 order); identity index into VectorStore + CsrGraph
Distance ordering single min-heap order for L2/InnerProduct/Cosine (IP/Cosine = negated similarity); NaN never enters
Degree bound NeighborList ≤ R; CsrGraph out-degree ≤ R; degree_rebalance enforces
Serialization stability magic+version header; CsrGraph / VectorStore / PQ-codebook layouts FROZEN; breaking change ⇒ version++
Trust boundary dim ≤ 4096, R ≤ 512, ef ≤ 4096, NaN/Inf rejected — enforced before any type is constructed

12. Phase 0 — Skeleton, exact-KNN baseline, and the recall harness

Foundation phase: the workspace stands up, the recall oracle (brute-force exact KNN) and RecallHarness exist before any approximate index, and a green CI prints a real recall@10 from a 1k-vector smoke graph. Every later phase is measured against this oracle.

0.1 Objectives

  • Scaffold the 7-crate Cargo workspace (ann-core, ann-graph, ann-build, ann-optimize, ann-search, ann-store, ann-cli) with the exact dep DAG from section 10; cargo build --workspace green, -D warnings.
  • Land VectorId, VectorStore, Metric, Distance, DistanceKernel (scalar only), DistanceCounter in ann-core.
  • Implement brute-force exact KNN = the recall oracle; build RecallHarness (ground-truth + recall@k formula) in ann-search.
  • Stub Graph/AdjacencyList/NeighborList/Layer/SearchContext/CsrGraph enough to compile + a trivial nearest-M graph + minimal greedy_search.
  • Seeded-RNG determinism harness; differential-oracle scaffold (hnswlib behind dev-only diff-hnswlib feature, default off).
  • CI (fmt + clippy + build + test) + Makefile + Dockerfile; smoke test reports a measurable recall@10 ≥ 0.95 on the nearest-M graph.

0.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

File Contents
Cargo.toml (root) [workspace] members=[7 ann-* crates], resolver="2", shared [workspace.dependencies] (smallvec, rand, rand_pcg, bitvec, tracing)
rust-toolchain.toml channel="1.78" (MSRV pin)
ann-core/src/lib.rs VectorId(u32), Metric{L2,InnerProduct,Cosine}, Distance(f32) (Ord via f32::total_cmp, NaN rejected)
ann-core/src/store.rs VectorStore{dim,data:Vec<f32>,len:u32}, row-major contiguous; get(VectorId)->&[f32] slices [i*dim..(i+1)*dim]
ann-core/src/kernel.rs trait DistanceKernel{fn distance(&self,a,b)->Distance;}; ScalarL2/ScalarIp/ScalarCosine; DistanceCounter{count:AtomicU64} inc()/load()
ann-core/src/oracle.rs exact_knn(store,&[f32],k,metric)->Vec<SearchResult>: full scan, bounded BinaryHeap cap k, O(n·dim)
ann-search/src/harness.rs RecallHarness{ground_truth:Vec<Vec<VectorId>>}; build(store,queries,k) precomputes exact KNN; recall_at_k(approx,gt)->f32
ann-graph/src/lib.rs stubs: NeighborList{ids,dists:SmallVec}, AdjacencyList, Layer, Graph, GraphKind, CsrGraph, EntryPoint
ann-search/src/lib.rs SearchParams{k,ef,beam_width}, VisitedSet (versioned bitset), SearchContext, SearchResult; minimal greedy_search
ann-cli/src/main.rs annopt clap stub: build/optimize/search/diagnose/tune/bench parse; bench --smoke runs path
ann-build,ann-optimize,ann-store crate shells, todo!() method stubs, compile only
tests/smoke.rs 1k×64 seeded vectors → nearest-M graph → greedy_search → recall@10
  • Dep DAG (enforced now): core ← {graph}; {core,graph} ← {build,search}; {core,graph,search} ← optimize; {core,graph} ← store; all ← cli. Reject cycles via cargo metadata CI check.
  • Distance convention (LOCKED): IP/Cosine converted to negated/1-sim Distance so smaller = closer uniformly; oracle + heaps share the convention.
  • Distance ordering: wrap f32::total_cmp; reject NaN/Inf at VectorStore ingest (Err(InvalidVector)) so heaps never see NaN.
  • VisitedSet: Vec<u32> stamp array + current:u32 generation; reset() bumps generation O(1); on u32 wrap → full clear.
  • Trivial graph (build_nearest_m): per node, oracle KNN(M+1), drop self, store NeighborList; this is the recall ceiling, not a real index.
  • Determinism: all randomness via rand_pcg::Pcg64 seeded from BuildParams.seed; no thread_rng, no HashMap iteration order on hot paths.

0.3 Deliverable

cargo build --workspace + cargo test --workspace green; annopt bench --smoke prints recall@10=<x> dist_comps=<n>; CI (fmt/clippy/build/test) passes on push; make ci and docker build reproduce locally; annopt --help lists all 6 subcommands.

0.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
cargo build/test --workspace exit 0
cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings clean
cargo fmt --check clean
Smoke: 1000×64, M=16, ef=64 recall@10 ≥ 0.95 (<0.90 = bug)

0.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input Expected
fixtures/tiny8.json 8 vectors, dim=2 exact_knn(q,k=3) → fixed id list, distances within 1e-6
fixtures/recall_cases.json (approx,gt) pairs recall@k ∈ {0.0,0.5,1.0} as labeled
fixtures/seed42_gt.bin 1k×64 seed=42 ground_truth hash matches golden
malformed dim_mismatch query len ≠ dim Err(DimMismatch), no panic
malformed nan_vector contains NaN Err(InvalidVector) at ingest
malformed k_gt_n k=20, n=8 returns 8 results, no panic

0.6 Pitfalls

  • NaN in distance heap: f32 PartialOrd leaves NaN unordered → BinaryHeap corrupts, silent wrong KNN. Root cause: raw f32 compare. Fix: total_cmp + reject NaN at ingest. Debug: assert heap monotonic.
  • Self in KNN: nearest-M includes the node itself (dist 0), wasting a slot and inflating recall. Fix: oracle returns k+1, builder drops self by id. Debug: assert edge i→i count == 0.
  • Recall convention flip: max-heap vs min-heap mismatch returns farthest. Root cause: missing Reverse on candidate heap. Debug: top result distance must be the smallest.
  • VisitedSet generation wrap: u32 overflow after 4B queries reuses stale stamps → false "visited". Fix: full clear on wrap. Debug: inject low-width gen in a test.
  • IP/Cosine sign: storing raw similarity (bigger=closer) breaks the universal "smaller=closer", inverting every downstream heap. Symptom: recall 0 only for IP/Cosine. Fix: negate at the kernel boundary; test all 3 metrics in the oracle.

0.7 Performance Concerns

  • Oracle is O(n·dim·q): 1k×64×100 queries ≈ 6.4M flops, sub-100ms scalar — fine as ground truth, never on the query path.
  • Ground-truth precompute for SIFT1M (1M×128) is O(n·q); cache as seed*_gt.bin, recompute only on seed/k change.
  • VectorStore row-major contiguous → sequential scan cache-friendly; 32-byte aligned alloc from day 0 so Phase 1 AVX2 loads need no realloc.
  • SmallVec<[_;R]> keeps small neighborhoods inline (no heap alloc) at typical R ≤ 64; spill boundary noted for Phase 2.
  • criterion dev-dep + benches/distance.rs stub wired so Phase 1 has a baseline slot.

0.8 Edge Cases

  • n=0 store → exact_knn returns empty, no panic; RecallHarness::build over empty queries → recall NaN flagged, not 0/0 panic.
  • k > n → return all n; recall denominator clamps to min(k,n).
  • dim=0 → rejected at ingest; dim=1 valid; single-vector dataset → KNN returns it.
  • Duplicate vectors (identical coords) → ties broken by VectorId ascending for determinism.
  • All-zero vectors under Cosine → division by zero → define dist as max finite, tested.
  • Query == stored vector → that id rank-0, distance 0.

0.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Response
Recall < 0.90 on smoke acceptance gate fail CI, dump mismatched ids
Non-deterministic gt seed-replay test fail; hunt non-seeded RNG / iteration order
Cargo cycle introduced cargo metadata graph check PR blocked
Dim mismatch at ingest Result Err(DimMismatch), no partial store
clippy warning -D warnings hard CI fail
hnswlib build absent feature off by default skip diff test, not fail
Toolchain drift > MSRV rust-toolchain.toml + MSRV CI leg fail fast

0.10 Operational Notes

  • make targets: fmt, lint, test, ci (= fmt-check + clippy-deny + build + test), bench, smoke, docker; single source of truth for local == CI.
  • Dockerfile: multi-stage pinned rust:1.78 (MSRV) builder → slim runtime; cargo build --workspace --locked; Cargo.lock committed; ENTRYPOINT ["annopt"]; image holds no datasets.
  • diff-hnswlib dev-only feature builds hnswlib via a sys/cc crate; absent toolchain → test #[cfg_attr(not(feature),ignore)], never breaks the default pipeline.
  • Logging via tracing behind RUST_LOG; bench emits a structured recall@10/dist_comps line for downstream Pareto plotting.
  • Artifacts cached: fixtures/seed*_gt.bin keyed by (seed,k,dim,metric); large ann-benchmarks data never committed (gated behind ANN_DATA_DIR, absent → skip + pass).

13. Phase 1 — SIMD distance kernels and the distance-computation budget

The distance kernel is the hot loop of every later phase: build, search, diagnostics, autotune all spend >90% of CPU here. This phase delivers correctness-by-oracle scalar kernels, runtime-dispatched SIMD (AVX2 / AVX-512 / NEON), the Metric → Distance convention, f32/f16/i8 datatypes, and the DistanceCounter that makes distance-computations/query the universal cost currency consumed by CostModel (Phase 11).

1.1 Objectives

  • Scalar oracle: l2_sqr / inner_product / cosine over &[f32]; bit-exact reference, never SIMD; all later kernels differential-tested against it.
  • SIMD kernels: AVX2 (8×f32), AVX-512 (16×f32, cfg-gated), NEON (4×f32); FMA accumulate; runtime is_x86_feature_detected! dispatch resolved ONCE per index, cached — no per-call branch.
  • metric_to_distance: L2 → squared L2 (skip sqrt, monotonic); InnerProduct/Cosine → negated similarity so smaller=closer holds across ALL heaps.
  • DataType: f32 primary; f16 (widen-to-f32) and i8 (PQ residual dequant) paths — callable stubs now, exact in Phase 12.
  • DistanceCounter: AtomicU64, +1 per kernel call via thread-local accumulate + batch flush; every later phase reports dist-comps/query.
  • Throughput: >2 GB/s on 128-dim L2 f32 (AVX2, single core); SIMD within fp tolerance of oracle.

1.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per P2-DistanceComputationIsTheUnitOfCost, ADR-09 SIMD kernels, ADR-08 distance convention, [B5] Fog+Intel Intrinsics Guide)

Files (all in ann-core):

File Owns
ann-core/src/metric.rs Metric, Distance, metric_to_distance()
ann-core/src/kernel/mod.rs DistanceKernel trait, make_kernel(Metric, DataType), dispatch table
ann-core/src/kernel/scalar.rs oracle l2_sqr / inner_product / cosine
ann-core/src/kernel/{avx2,avx512,neon}.rs SIMD kernels: #[target_feature(enable="avx2,fma")]; avx512f (cfg-gated); #[cfg(target_arch="aarch64")] NEON
ann-core/src/datatype.rs DataType, f16/i8 widen + dequant helpers
ann-core/src/counter.rs DistanceCounter

Interfaces:

pub enum DataType { F32, F16, I8 }
pub trait DistanceKernel: Send + Sync {
    fn distance(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> Distance; // counter bumped inside
    fn dim(&self) -> usize;
}
pub fn metric_to_distance(m: Metric, raw: f32) -> Distance; // L2->raw(sqr); IP->-raw; Cos->-raw_norm
pub fn make_kernel(m: Metric, dt: DataType) -> Box<dyn DistanceKernel>;
  • Dispatch: detect ISA at first make_kernel; pick AVX512 > AVX2 > NEON > Scalar; store concrete struct (L2Avx2, IpAvx2, …) holding fn-ptr — gate every unsafe kernel behind is_x86_feature_detected!.
  • AVX2 L2: 4 independent __m256 accumulators (break FMA dep chain), _mm256_fmadd_ps((a-b),(a-b),acc), horizontal-sum + scalar remainder loop for dim % 8. IP analogous (fma(a,b,acc)). NEON: float32x4_t ×4, vfmaq_f32.
  • Cosine: precompute & cache per-vector norms in VectorStore (Phase-0 VectorStore gains norms: Vec<f32>); normalize at insert, kernel degrades to IP on normalized input — never renormalize per call.
  • VectorStore.data rows padded/aligned 32B (AVX2) / 64B (AVX-512); kernels use loadu (unaligned, ~free) to avoid downstream alignment constraints.
  • Counter wiring: distance() bumps a thread-local u64, flush to AtomicU64 every 1024 or on snapshot().

1.3 Deliverable

ann-core exposes make_kernel, all three metrics × {scalar, AVX2, AVX-512, NEON}, the DistanceCounter, and a criterion suite (benches/distance.rs) printing GB/s + dist-comps. annopt bench --kernels reports chosen ISA + throughput per metric/dim. cargo test -p ann-core green on x86_64 + aarch64.

1.4 Acceptance Criteria

Check Target
SIMD vs scalar oracle |simd-scalar| <= 1e-4 * max(1,|scalar|), 10⁴ random pairs, dims {1,7,8,15,16,128,768,1536}
Throughput L2 f32 dim-128 AVX2 ≥ 2 GB/s; dim-768 ≥ 1.5 GB/s single core
Monotonicity rank(distance) == rank(true nearest) per metric on 1k fixture
DistanceCounter exactly 1 per distance() call (N calls → snapshot==N); <1% release overhead
Scalar fallback -Ctarget-feature=-avx2 build still passes correctness via dispatch fallback

1.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Contents Expected
fixtures/kernel/pairs_f32.tsv 200 dim-128 pairs golden L2sqr/IP/Cos per row (1e-6)
fixtures/kernel/tails.tsv dims 1,7,15,17,127 tail-handling exactness
fixtures/kernel/norms.tsv unit + zero-norm vectors cosine zero-norm → sentinel, not NaN
fixtures/kernel/bad_dim.tsv a.len != b.len debug_assert/Err, never UB
fixtures/kernel/nan_inf.tsv NaN/±Inf components NaN propagates to Distance; no silent 0

Malformed: length mismatch, empty slice (dim 0), subnormals, all-zero vector under cosine → Distance(0.0) sentinel + flag (maximally dissimilar), never crash.

1.6 Pitfalls

  • Cosine zero-norm div-by-0 → NaN poisons heap ordering. Root: unnormalized zero/empty vector. Debug: assert finite; sentinel Distance(0.0).
  • Single SIMD accumulator → latency-bound on FMA dep chain (~4cyc), 3–4× slowdown. Root: no ILP. Debug: perf stat IPC <1 → use 4 accumulators.
  • #[target_feature] on wrong CPU → SIGILL. Root: AVX-512 fn without detection, or target-cpu=native shipped with runtime dispatch. Debug: dispatch via detected enum only; never #[inline(always)] SIMD into a scalar caller.
  • f16→f32 widen rounding drifts from oracle. Root: oracle on raw f16. Fix: oracle consumes the SAME widened f32 slice.
  • DistanceCounter atomic contention under N readers → ~30% search slowdown. Root: fetch_add per call. Fix: thread-local accumulate + batch flush.
  • L2 sqrt drift / catastrophic cancellation in Σ(a-b)² for large near-equal vectors → tiny-negative under FMA reorder. Fix: squared-L2 everywhere, clamp ≥0, relative (not absolute) tolerance.

1.7 Performance Concerns

  • 4× unrolled FMA accumulators to hide FMA latency; single accumulator caps ~¼ peak.
  • L2 normalization at insert (one-time), not per query; no f64 promotion on hot path.
  • Avoid sqrt/div in loop (squared L2, precomputed cosine norms); prefetch next row (dim*4 bytes ahead) in linear scans.
  • Dispatch cost amortized: ISA chosen once per index, not per query.
  • f16: _mm256_cvtph_ps widen 8×f16→8×f32 — ~½ bandwidth saving, measure not assume; bench loadu vs aligned (expect <2%).

1.8 Edge Cases

  • dim 0 → Distance(0.0), no loop; dim < lane width → pure scalar tail.
  • dim not multiple of lane width → masked/scalar remainder (% 8 / % 16 / % 4).
  • Identical vectors → L2sqr exactly 0.0; cosine exactly 0.0 after negate.
  • Negative IP (anti-correlated) → positive Distance after negation; ordering preserved.
  • Duplicate vectors → ties broken by VectorId ascending for reproducible fixtures.
  • Very high dim (1536, 4096 cap) → f32 sum error grows ~√dim, < 1e-4 rel, alloc-free; documented.

1.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
SIMD ≠ scalar beyond tol differential test in CI gate merge; bisect ISA path
SIGILL on deploy host is_x86_feature_detected! mismatch runtime fallback to scalar, log ISA once
NaN in distance NaN-aware comparator (NaN → +∞ in heaps) propagate, flag query degenerate
Tolerance flake across uarch relative tol + fixed seed 0xA11CE document FMA-order nondeterminism
Counter overflow u64 wrap (infeasible <2⁶⁴) assert in long bench

1.10 Operational Notes

  • annopt bench --kernels prints chosen ISA, GB/s, dist-comps; regression gate vs baselines/kernel.json (±5%).
  • CI matrix: x86_64 (avx2, avx512 via qemu/feature-mask), aarch64 NEON, scalar-forced (-avx2).
  • RUST_LOG=ann_core::kernel=debug logs detected ISA once at startup for field diagnosis of slow nodes.
  • Ship release artifacts with runtime dispatch (no target-cpu=native).
  • DistanceCounter is the canonical cost unit for CostModel (Phase 11) — never reset mid-query; snapshot() brackets each query in RecallHarness.
  • Fixtures are the contract: any kernel change must re-pass pairs_f32.tsv + tails.tsv before merge.

14. Phase 2 — Graph representation (mutable build form + immutable CSR)

2.1 Objectives

  • Define the navigable-graph data structures in ann-graph: NeighborList, AdjacencyList, Layer, mutable Graph (GraphKind::{Hnsw,Vamana}), immutable CsrGraph.
  • NeighborList: capped at R, sorted ascending by Distance, worst-eviction on overflow — invariant enforced at every mutation.
  • Freeze step Graph -> CsrGraph (offsets+edges; layer-0 adjacency only, the search hot path).
  • Versioned binary (de)serialization for Graph and CsrGraph; round-trip bit-identical adjacency.
  • NON-GOAL: no build algorithms (Phase 3+), no search (Phase 5), no mmap/PQ (Phase 12). Pure container + layout layer. std.

2.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per P4-OptimizedGraphImmutableSendSync, ADR-04 build-vs-search form, ADR-05 NeighborList container, INV-1 sorted+capped, INV-12 CSR well-formedness)

Files under ann-graph/src/ (per section 10 repo layout):

File Contents
neighbor.rs NeighborList { ids: SmallVec<[VectorId; R]>, dists: SmallVec<[Distance; R]> }, try_insert, truncate_to, worst, contains
adjacency.rs AdjacencyList { neighbors: Vec<NeighborList> }, index = VectorId
layer.rs Layer { adjacency: AdjacencyList, level: u8 }
graph.rs Graph, GraphKind, EntryPoint, mutators add_node, add_edge_directed, neighbors
csr.rs CsrGraph { offsets, edges, entry_point }, Graph::freeze, CsrGraph::neighbors
serde.rs write_graph/read_graph, write_csr/read_csr, MAGIC, FORMAT_VERSION, GraphError

Signatures:

impl NeighborList {
    fn try_insert(&mut self, id: VectorId, d: Distance, cap: u32) -> bool; // false if rejected (full & d >= worst)
    fn worst(&self) -> Option<Distance>;        // last element = max dist
    fn truncate_to(&mut self, cap: u32);        // keep cap closest
}
impl Graph {
    fn add_node(&mut self, top_level: u8) -> VectorId;
    fn add_edge_directed(&mut self, from: VectorId, to: VectorId, d: Distance, level: u8, cap: u32);
    fn neighbors(&self, id: VectorId, level: u8) -> &NeighborList;
    fn freeze(&self) -> CsrGraph;               // layer-0 adjacency -> CSR
}
impl CsrGraph { fn neighbors(&self, id: VectorId) -> &[VectorId]; } // edges[offsets[id] .. offsets[id+1]]
  • try_insert: binary-search ascending position, shift ids+dists together; if len==cap && d >= worst, reject; else insert + drop last when overflowing.
  • freeze: prefix-sum offsets[0..=n] from neighbors[i].len(); flatten ids into edges; assert offsets[n] == edges.len().
  • SmallVec inline cap R (typedef R = 64) → low-degree nodes stay heap-free; HNSW upper layers store sparse rows.

2.3 Deliverable

  • ann-graph compiling with the full type set; integration test build_freeze_roundtrip: construct a hand graph (≈8 nodes, known edges), freeze(), write_csr+read_csr, assert offsets/edges/entry_point identical; also round-trip mutable Graph and assert per-(node,level) NeighborList equality across GraphKind variants.

2.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • NeighborList always sorted asc, len ≤ cap, no duplicate VectorId — debug_assert + property test (10k random ops).
  • freeze: offsets.len()==n+1, monotonic non-decreasing, offsets[n]==edges.len(); CsrGraph::neighbors(id) ids match Graph::neighbors(id,0) order.
  • Serialization byte-identical (read(write(x))==x) for Graph and CsrGraph, all GraphKind.
  • Bad magic / unknown FORMAT_VERSION / truncated body → typed GraphError (never panic).
  • freeze of 1M nodes × R=64 < 50 ms; CSR bytes ≈ 4*(n+1) + 4*E within 2% of formula.

2.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Content Expected
graph_8node_hnsw.json 8 nodes, Hnsw, 2 layers, explicit edges freeze → golden offsets/edges, degree histogram
graph_vamana.json Vamana, R=32, single layer round-trip adjacency + entry_point equal
csr_8node.bin golden serialized CsrGraph read_csr deep-equals frozen graph (SHA-256 match)
bad_magic.bin wrong 4-byte magic Err(GraphError::BadMagic)
future_version.bin FORMAT_VERSION+1 Err(GraphError::UnsupportedVersion)
truncated.bin header claims N edges, body short Err(GraphError::Truncated)
offset_oob.bin non-monotonic / offsets[n]!=edges.len() Err(GraphError::CorruptOffsets)
edge_oob.bin edge id ≥ N Err(GraphError::CorruptEdge)

2.6 Pitfalls

  • Unsorted insert → search/worst() assume asc order → wrong distance bound → silent recall loss. Root: append without binary-search. Debug: assert sortedness in try_insert.
  • Eviction keeps worst → off-by-one at len==cap; closer node dropped. Debug: test inserting closer node into full list, assert farthest evicted.
  • offsets u32 overflow → n*R ≥ 2^32 total edges. Guard: checked prefix-sum → CorruptOffsets/error at freeze.
  • SmallVec spill churn → inline cap < typical degree → per-node heap alloc on build. Fix: inline cap = R. Detect: alloc-count micro-bench.
  • Endianness drift → serialize fixed little-endian; BE host without conversion corrupts ids. Debug: golden bin cross-arch CI.
  • Self-loop / dup edge → add_edge_directed(p,p) or repeat inflates degree, wastes hops. Guard: reject from==to, dedup via contains on insert.

2.7 Performance Concerns

  • CsrGraph::neighbors = two offsets loads + contiguous slice → 1-2 cache lines, prefetch-friendly; the search-serving layout.
  • Mutable AdjacencyList is alloc-scattered (Vec<NeighborList>) — fine at build, never on search path; always freeze before serving.
  • try_insert O(R) shift; R≤64 stays register/cache-friendly; binary search bounds comparisons to log R.
  • freeze single O(N+E) pass, no per-node alloc; write_csr is a POD slice copy of offsets+edges, not per-node serialization.

2.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty graph (N=0): freeze → offsets=[0], edges=[]; round-trip valid.
  • Zero-neighbor node: offsets[i]==offsets[i+1], empty slice (legal; reachability owned by Phase 9).
  • Single node: entry_point = that node, no edges.
  • cap==0: try_insert always rejects (degenerate but legal).
  • Hnsw with one populated layer → freeze still flattens layer 0.
  • Hnsw upper layer with no node at that level → Layer of all-empty NeighborList rows.

2.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Response
Non-monotonic offsets freeze/read_csr validation Err(CorruptOffsets)
Edge id ≥ N post-load range scan Err(CorruptEdge), no serve
Degree > cap add_edge_directed / debug_assert Err(DegreeExceeded)
Bad magic / version skew header read BadMagic / UnsupportedVersion
entry_point ≥ N read_* validate Err(BadEntryPoint)
Partial write (crash mid-serialize) footer length/checksum mismatch Truncated; writer uses atomic temp+rename

Validation tiering: trusted internal freeze skips the full scan; read_* of external bytes runs the offsets+edge-range scan once before returning.

2.10 Operational Notes

  • MAGIC = b"ANNG" (Graph) / b"ANNC" (CsrGraph); FORMAT_VERSION bumped on any layout change — refuse mismatch, no silent migration.
  • Header (fixed-size, little-endian): magic, version, GraphKind, N, max_degree, Metric, entry_point; CSR section length-prefixed and self-delimiting (nests cleanly for Phase 12 mmap).
  • Write is atomic (temp file + rename) so a crash never leaves a half-written index for the Phase 12 mmap loader.
  • annopt diagnose --dump-csr prints offsets/edges + degree histogram (min/mean/p50/max) — eyeball degree distribution before Phase 7 diagnostics exist.
  • Every external load path validates — never trust on-disk bytes; corrupt input → typed GraphError, never UB.

15. Phase 3 — HNSW construction (base: layers, greedy insert, ef_construction)

3.1 Objectives

  • Implement HnswBuilder base path in ann-build: level assignment, descending greedy entry, per-layer candidate gathering, naive nearest-M neighbor selection (select_neighbors_heuristic deferred to Phase 4).
  • Produce a multi-layer Graph { kind: GraphKind::Hnsw { m, m_max0, ef_construction, ml } } over up to 100k vectors that out-recalls the Phase-0 trivial/random graph at equal ef.
  • LOCKED scope: NO heuristic selection, NO M_max re-prune of touched neighbors (Phase 4), NO Vamana (Phase 6). Insert deterministic given BuildParams.seed. Keep selection a single call site so Phase 4 swaps nearest_m → select_neighbors_heuristic in one place.

3.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per [P1] HNSW paper §3–4 layered insertion + level assignment, P8-DeterminismViaSeededRng, ADR-06 heuristic selection placeholder, INV-4 HNSW layer containment)

Files (§10 layout): ann-build/src/hnsw.rs (HnswBuilder, insert loop), ann-build/src/level.rs (RNG + level draw), ann-build/src/params.rs (BuildParams). Reuses ann-graph::{Graph,Layer,AdjacencyList,NeighborList,GraphKind}, ann-core::{VectorStore,VectorId,Metric,Distance,DistanceKernel,DistanceCounter}. A thin local search_layer (full engine = Phase 5).

pub struct HnswBuilder<'a> { store: &'a VectorStore, kernel: &'a dyn DistanceKernel,
    params: BuildParams, rng: SplitMix64 /* seeded from params.seed */ }
impl<'a> HnswBuilder<'a> {
    pub fn new(store, kernel, params) -> Self;
    pub fn build(&mut self) -> Graph;          // inserts VectorId 0..len in order
    fn assign_level(&mut self) -> u8;          // L = floor(-ln(U) * ml), U in (0,1]
    fn insert(&mut self, g: &mut Graph, q: VectorId);
}
fn search_layer(g:&Graph, store:&VectorStore, ker:&dyn DistanceKernel,
    q:&[f32], eps:&[VectorId], ef:u32, level:u8, ctx:&mut SearchContext) -> Vec<(Distance,VectorId)>;
fn nearest_m(w:&[(Distance,VectorId)], m:u32) -> NeighborList; // w sorted asc → take m
  • Level draw (level.rs): ml = 1.0 / (m as f32).ln() (LOCKED ml=1/ln M); u = next_f32_open01(rng) (exclude 0 → no +inf); L = (-(u.ln()) * ml).floor() as u8, clamp < 64. Distribution P(level≥l)=exp(-l/ml); ~1/M promote per level.
  • First node: becomes entry_point; g.layers sized L+1; empty NeighborLists.
  • insert(q): Lq = assign_level(); top = g.layers.len()-1; ep = entry_point.
    • Descend (level = top .. Lq+1): w = search_layer(ep, ef=1, level); carry nearest as next ep. No linking.
    • Link (level = min(Lq,top) ..= 0): w = search_layer(ep, ef=ef_construction, level); M_l = if level==0 {m_max0} else {m}; sel = nearest_m(w, M_l); add bidirectional edges q↔n for n in sel, n != q; sorted-asc insert into each NeighborList, capped at R (overflow dropped — re-prune is Phase 4). ep = w[0].1 for next-lower level.
    • If Lq > top: append empty Layers up to Lq, then after link phase set entry_point = q.
  • search_layer: candidate heap seeded with eps; pop closest-unexpanded; push unvisited neighbors at level (filter via VisitedSet); maintain result set of best ef; stop when min(unexpanded) > max(results). Each kernel.distance bumps DistanceCounter.
  • State reuse: one SearchContext (versioned VisitedSet, O(1) epoch reset) reused across all inserts → no steady-state alloc.

3.3 Deliverable

HnswBuilder::build → serializable Graph (Phase-2 mutable form) over 100k f32 vectors. annopt build --algo hnsw -M 16 --m-max0 32 --ef-construction 200 --seed 1 writes it; reload + Phase-0 brute greedy_search returns top-k.

3.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
recall@10 vs Phase-0 random graph, equal ef=64 (SIFT-10k analog) HNSW ≥ +0.30 absolute higher
recall@10 @ ef=64 on 100k ≥ 0.85 (pre-heuristic; Phase 4 → ≥0.95)
build 100k @ M=16, ef_construction=200 < 90 s single-thread
determinism byte-identical graph across runs at fixed seed; thread-count-invariant (single-thread build)
degree invariant out-degree ≤ m_max0 on L0, ≤ m above; no self-loop; no dup neighbor id
reachability every node reachable from entry_point on L0
level histogram geometric: ~N/M above L0, ×(1/M) per level, ±15%

3.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Input Expected
fixtures/hnsw/levels_seed42.json seed=42, M=16, 100k draws level histogram + first 16 level values exact
fixtures/hnsw/insert_trace_8.json 8 hand-placed 2-D pts, M=3 per-insert ep descent path + final adjacency lists
fixtures/hnsw/graph_1k_M8.bin 1k, M=8, seed=1 golden serialized bytes; byte-equal on rebuild
fixtures/hnsw/recall_10k_ef64.json 10k built graph {recall@10 ≥ 0.85, random_baseline ≤ 0.5}
fixtures/hnsw/degree_caps.json built graph max/avg degree per layer ≤ caps

Malformed: m=0 or m=1 → BuildParamError::BadM (ml = 1/ln{0,1} = -inf/inf); ef_construction=0 → error; empty store (len=0) → empty Graph + sentinel entry_point, no panic; NaN/Inf vector → reject at insert; duplicate VectorId → error.

3.6 Pitfalls

  • U=0 in level draw → ln(0)=-inf → level u8::MAX → giant layer stack. Fix: open-interval (0,1] RNG; assert L < 64.
  • EP promoted before linking → new top node has no edges → first query stalls/empty results. Order: link, then set entry_point.
  • Missing reverse edge (only q→n, not n→q) → orphans, unreachable from EP, recall holes. Debug: degree histogram + reachability after each 1k inserts.
  • Self-loop / dup: search_layer can return q or an existing neighbor; filter n != q and dedup by VectorId before insert; symptom = inflated degree counts.
  • VisitedSet not epoch-reset between inserts → false "visited", missed candidates, silent recall drop. Fix: generation-stamped bitset, O(1) bump; handle u32 epoch wrap (full reset).
  • ef_construction < m_max0 → candidate pool < M_l → under-connected L0. Assert ef_construction ≥ m_max0.
  • Tie-break by distance only → nondeterministic neighbor sets on equal dists. Fix: secondary key VectorId.

3.7 Performance Concerns

  • Insert i ≈ ef_construction × mean_degree × hops distance evals; total build ≈ N·ef_construction·M·dim FLOPs — bound by SIMD DistanceKernel throughput (Phase 1, >5 GFLOP/s). Budget ≤ ~200k distance-comps/insert avg to hit 90 s.
  • Reuse SearchContext per insert → zero heap churn; NeighborList SmallVec keeps lists inline.
  • Versioned bitset VisitedSet (O(1) clear) beats HashSet ~3–5×.
  • Mutable AdjacencyList (Vec indexed by VectorId) random-access; CSR freeze is Phase-2/optimize, not here.

3.8 Edge Cases

  • N=0 → empty graph, sentinel EP; N=1 → single node, no edges, it is EP.
  • All-identical vectors (all dist=0) → ties stable by VectorId; graph connected; recall trivially 1.0; caps still enforced.
  • N < M → degree naturally < M; no prune; no over-connection attempt.
  • k or ef > len → clamp; return all sorted.
  • Node promoted ≥2 levels above top → insert empty intermediate layers; only this node occupies them until others promote — no panic on empty upper layers.

3.9 Failure Analysis

  • Recall collapse to baseline → broken descend (ef≠1, wrong layer, or frontier not re-seeded from eps) ⇒ effectively random graph. Detect via recall_10k_ef64 fixture; assert monotone nearest-dist per descended layer.
  • Disconnected component → missing bidirectional link ⇒ unreachable from EP. Transient upper-layer isolation OK; permanent orphan is a bug (Phase 9 connect_components repairs later, but assert L0 reachability here).
  • Degree-cap drop removing the only path → connectivity loss; root cause = naive truncation ignoring connectivity (known limitation → Phase 4 heuristic keep_pruned).
  • Build hang → wrong stop condition (>= vs >) ⇒ infinite re-expansion. Cap expansions = len; panic with diagnostic if exceeded.
  • Nondeterminism → unseeded RNG or HashMap iteration in hot path ⇒ byte-diff fixtures fail.
  • Recovery: build is pure in-memory; on panic discard partial graph and restart (no on-disk mutation until Phase 12 save_graph).

3.10 Operational Notes

  • annopt build --algo hnsw -M <m> --m-max0 <2m> --ef-construction <ef_c> --seed <s>; defaults M=16, m_max0=32, ef_construction=200; ml/m_max0 derive from M, overridable.
  • annopt diagnose <graph> (Phase 7 preview) / build log: order, max_layer, level histogram, per-layer degree min/avg/max, entry_point, total distance-comps (from DistanceCounter), wall-time, peak RSS; log per-10k-inserts progress + inserts/s.
  • --verify runs post-build reachability + degree-bound asserts before serialize.
  • Regression gate (Phase 13): build_seconds, avg_degree_L0, level_count — fail CI on >10% drift.
  • Non-goal reminder: edge quality is intentionally weak here; do NOT tune for production recall until Phase 4 heuristic + Phase 8 optimizer passes.

16. Phase 4 — Select-neighbors heuristic, bidirectional linking, M_max pruning

The HNSW edge-quality / navigability core. Replaces Phase 3's naive nearest-M selection with the diversity heuristic (Malkov & Yashunin Algorithm 4), wires symmetric edges, and bounds out-degree by re-pruning over-full neighborhoods. Lives in ann-build; mutates the build-form Graph (AdjacencyList/NeighborList); read by ann-search later.

4.1 Objectives

  • Implement select_neighbors_heuristic(candidates, m, extend, keep_pruned) -> NeighborList exactly per Algorithm 4: keep candidate e only if d(e,q) < d(e,r) for every already-selected r (occlusion test).
  • Replace the truncate-to-M selection in HnswBuilder insert (Phase 3) with the heuristic on every layer.
  • Add bidirectional edges: for each selected neighbor n of new node q, also add q into n's NeighborList.
  • Enforce out-degree ≤ M_max (layer>0) / M_max0 (layer 0): when an insert pushes n over cap, re-run the heuristic over n's full neighborhood + the new edge to pick survivors.
  • Prove measurable win: recall@10 ↑ and mean distance-comps/query ↓ vs Phase 3 at equal ef, via RecallHarness.

4.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per [P1] Algorithm 4 select-neighbors heuristic, P7-NavigabilityOverRawNearestM, ADR-06 HNSW edge selection, INV-1 sorted+capped, INV-10 prune/heuristic termination)

File Change
ann-build/src/select.rs New: select_neighbors_heuristic, extend_candidates helper, keep_pruned backfill.
ann-build/src/hnsw.rs HnswBuilder::insert — swap naive truncate(m) for heuristic; add link_bidirectional; add prune_neighbor.
ann-graph/src/neighbor.rs NeighborList::insert_sorted(id, dist), len(), is_full(cap), into_candidates().

Algorithm 4 (pseudo-code, candidates ascending by d(.,q)):

fn select_neighbors_heuristic(C: [(id,d_q)], M, extend, keep_pruned) -> NeighborList:
    if extend: C += {neighbors-of-each-c} (dedup, recompute d_q)   # extendCandidates
    R = []                                  # result, sorted asc
    Wd = []                                 # discarded (for keep_pruned backfill)
    for (e, d_eq) in C ascending:
        if |R| == M: break
        occluded = any( dist(e, r) < d_eq for r in R )   # r closer to e than q is
        if !occluded: R.push(e)
        else if keep_pruned: Wd.push((e,d_eq))
    if keep_pruned:
        while |R| < M and Wd: R.push(Wd.pop_closest())
    return NeighborList::from_sorted(R)
  • extend=false, keep_pruned=true are the HNSW defaults (matches hnswlib getNeighborsByHeuristic2).
  • dist(e,r) = data-distance via ann-core DistanceKernel; counts against DistanceCounter.
  • Bidirectional link: link_bidirectional(q, selected, layer) → for each n: adj[n].insert_sorted(q, d(q,n)); if adj[n].len() > cap(layer) call prune_neighbor(n, layer).
  • prune_neighbor(n, layer): take adj[n].into_candidates() (already has distances to n), run select_neighbors_heuristic(.., cap, extend=false, keep_pruned=true), replace adj[n].

4.3 Deliverable

HnswBuilder producing a heuristic-selected, bidirectionally-linked, degree-bounded HNSW graph. annopt build --selector heuristic builds; annopt diagnose (Phase 7 stub now) prints mean out-degree ≤ M_max0. Unit + bench tests green.

4.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
Out-degree invariant max out-degree(layer 0) ≤ M_max0, ≤ M_max for layer>0, ∀ nodes, post-build.
recall@10 vs Phase 3 ≥ +3 pp at equal ef (e.g. naive 0.88 → heuristic ≥ 0.91) on 100k SIFT-analog.
dist-comps/query ≤ 0.95× Phase 3 at equal recall@10 (navigability → fewer hops).
Symmetry sanity for sampled q: ≥ 95% of selected edges have reverse edge present (modulo prune eviction).
Determinism fixed seed ⇒ byte-identical CsrGraph across runs.

4.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input Expected
heuristic_3pt.json 3 candidates, M=2, one occluded selects 2 non-occluded; occluded one in keep_pruned backfill only if `
heuristic_colinear.json colinear points, ties in distance stable order by (d_q, VectorId); deterministic.
bidir_link.json insert sequence, M=4 golden adjacency dump; reverse edges present.
mmax_overflow.json node forced to M_max+1 edges post-prune len == M_max0; survivors == heuristic output.
Malformed: empty_candidates C=[] returns empty NeighborList, no panic.
Malformed: m_zero M=0 returns empty; debug_assert(m>0) in non-fixture build.

4.6 Pitfalls

  • Occlusion direction flipped: comparing d(e,q) vs d(r,q) instead of d(e,r) → degenerates to nearest-M, recall flat. Debug: assert heuristic output ≠ truncate(M) on heuristic_3pt.
  • Self-loop: q selecting itself or n getting edge to itself during bidir link → skip id==self. Symptom: degree off-by-one, infinite expand in search.
  • Prune evicts the just-added edge: re-pruning n may drop q legitimately — correct, but breaks naive symmetry assertions. Allow ≤5% asymmetry; never assert exact symmetry.
  • extend_candidates distance double-count: re-adding neighbors-of-candidates without dedup inflates DistanceCounter and skews cost model. Dedup by VectorId before scoring.
  • Re-prune recursion: prune_neighbor triggering further prunes if it adds edges — it must only remove, never add. Pitfall: calling link_bidirectional from inside prune.
  • Distance recompute drift: into_candidates() reuses stored Distance; if a stale distance leaks (wrong base node), heuristic occludes wrong edges. Always distances-to-n, not to q.

4.7 Performance Concerns

  • Heuristic is O(M·|C|) distance comps (each candidate vs each selected) — at ef_construction=200, M=16 → ~3200 extra comps/insert. Acceptable at build time; never on search path.
  • prune_neighbor fires per over-full neighbor: hot-node fan-in can re-prune the same node repeatedly/insert. Batch: prune once after all bidir links for the insert, not per-edge.
  • dist(e,r) reuses already-computed pairwise distances where possible; cache within one select call (small M×M scratch). SIMD kernel from Phase 1.
  • NeighborList is SmallVec<[_; R]> → no heap alloc for typical R≤64; insert_sorted is O(R) shift, fine at build time.

4.8 Edge Cases

  • |C| ≤ M: all non-occluded kept; backfill fills rest; never exceeds M.
  • All candidates mutually occluded (tight cluster): R may end < M without keep_pruned; with it, backfill to M.
  • Duplicate vectors (d=0): occlusion d(e,r)=0 < d_eq evicts one of a dup pair — desirable (no redundant edge); handle 0<0 false correctly.
  • First inserts (graph < M nodes): cap effectively min(M, n-1); no panic on small graph.
  • Layer above node's max level: node absent from those layers; bidir link only within shared layers.

4.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Root cause Detection
Degree blowup prune_neighbor not called / wrong cap on layer post-build invariant scan; diagnose out-degree hist.
Recall regression vs Phase 3 heuristic mis-implemented / occlusion inverted RecallHarness A/B gate in CI.
Disconnected hub aggressive pruning orphans low-fan-in nodes orphan_count (Phase 7); deferred repair → Phase 8/9.
Non-determinism HashMap iteration in extend/dedup use sorted / BTreeMap or sort by VectorId; seed test.

4.10 Operational Notes

  • CLI flags: --selector {naive|heuristic} (default heuristic), --keep-pruned, --extend-candidates (default off) — keep naive for differential A/B.
  • Log per-build: mean/max out-degree, prune-eviction count, total dist-comps — feeds Phase 11 CostModel.
  • Differential target: edge sets within tolerance of hnswlib getNeighborsByHeuristic2 (same seed, same params); store golden adjacency for regression.
  • Invariant max out-degree ≤ M_max0 is a CI gate; violation fails the build, not just a warning.

17. Phase 5 — Search engine (greedy/beam best-first, ef control, recall@k)

Implements ann-search: the best-first traversal that turns a navigable graph + a query vector into top-k. Center of the latency budget; every distance computation here is counted against DistanceCounter. greedy_search walks one HNSW layer; beam_search walks the flat CsrGraph (Vamana + optimized HNSW base). RecallHarness certifies recall@k. Non-goal: optimization passes (Phase 8–11) — search here treats the graph as immutable.

5.1 Objectives

  • greedy_search(g, q, ep, ef, ctx) — single-layer best-first; working list size ef; candidate min-heap, result max-heap; correct stop condition.
  • HNSW full path: descend layers top..1 at ef=1, then search-layer layer 0 at ef; return top-k.
  • beam_search(g: &CsrGraph, q, params, ctx) — beam_width expansions/iteration over CSR; identical convergence, better cache locality.
  • VisitedSet: versioned bitset, O(1) per-query reset (no realloc, no full memset).
  • SearchContext reuse: zero steady-state heap allocation across queries (heaps + visited cleared, not freed).
  • RecallHarness: recall@k vs exact KNN; report mean distance-comps + p50/p95/p99 latency.
  • Hooks: batch_search (query slice, reused ctx pool), filtered_search (predicate over VectorId).
  • Target: recall@10 ≥ 0.95 on a 1M graph at a reported ef.

5.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per [P1] Algorithm 2 search-layer stop condition, [P7] ann-benchmarks recall@k protocol, P1-RecallMeasuredNeverAssumed, ADR-10 visited tracking, ADR-17 recall measurement, INV-7 pure-read immutable search, INV-8 VisitedSet per-query reset)

Files (ann-search/src/): lib.rs, context.rs (SearchContext, VisitedSet), greedy.rs (greedy_search, search_layer), beam.rs (beam_search), params.rs (SearchParams), recall.rs (RecallHarness), batch.rs, filter.rs.

VisitedSet — versioned: marks: Vec<u32>, gen: u32. visited(id)=marks[id]==gen; mark(id)=marks[id]=gen; reset()=gen+=1 (O(1)); on gen wrap to 0 do one marks.fill(0) then gen=1.

SearchContext: visited: VisitedSet, candidates: BinaryHeap<Reverse<(Distance,VectorId)>> (min-heap), results: BinaryHeap<(Distance,VectorId)> (max-heap, holds best ef). clear()=candidates.clear(); results.clear(); visited.reset() — retains capacity.

search_layer(layer, q, eps, ef, ctx) (HNSW Algorithm 2):

for e in eps: d=dist(q,e); mark(e); push candidates(d,e); push results(d,e)
loop:
  (cd,c)=candidates.pop()                    // nearest unexpanded
  if cd > results.peek().0: break            // stop: closest cand worse than worst result
  for n in layer.neighbors(c):
    if visited(n): continue
    mark(n); dn=dist(q,n)
    if dn < results.peek().0 || results.len()<ef:
      push candidates(dn,n); push results(dn,n)
      if results.len()>ef: results.pop()     // evict worst

greedy_search = search_layer on a Layer. HNSW descend: ep=g.entry_point; for lvl in (1..=top).rev(): ep=search_layer(lvl,ep,ef=1)[0]; then search_layer(0,ep,ef); drain results → sorted Vec<SearchResult>, truncate to k.

beam_search over CsrGraph: neighbors = edges[offsets[id]..offsets[id+1]] (contiguous, prefetchable); pop beam_width nearest unexpanded per iteration, expand all, batch distance calls; same stop condition. beam_width=1 ≡ greedy; larger trades nothing in dist-comps but shortens wall-clock via ILP/SIMD batching.

SearchParams { k, ef, beam_width }; invariant ef >= k. Distances via ann-core DistanceKernel; increment DistanceCounter per dist().

5.3 Deliverable

ann-search compiling green. annopt search --index <g> --queries <q> --k 10 --ef <E> returns ids + distances. RecallHarness report: recall@10, mean dist-comps/query, p50/p95/p99 µs. Smoke: search the Phase 0 1k graph; full: 1M graph at recall@10 ≥ 0.95.

5.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
recall@10 @ reported ef, 1M graph ≥ 0.95
ef=k lower bound exactly k results, sorted asc by Distance
Steady-state allocs/query (batch) 0 (allocator counter verified)
VisitedSet.reset() O(1), no per-query memset except gen-wrap
greedy vs beam(width=1) bit-identical result sets
Results ordering strictly ascending Distance, no dup ids
ef monotonicity recall non-decreasing as ef↑ (same graph/query)

5.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Contents / expected
tiny_line_graph.json 8 colinear pts, R=2; query mid → deterministic exact top-3
1k_smoke.graph + 1k_queries.fvecs recall@10 ≥ 0.99 at ef=64
gt_1k.ivecs exact KNN ground truth for RecallHarness
ef_sweep.expected recall per ef ∈ {10,16,32,64,128}: monotone non-decreasing
disconnected.graph (malformed) EP cannot reach a node set → recall < 1.0, MUST NOT panic/loop
empty_neighbors.graph (malformed) node with 0 out-edges mid-search → terminates, partial result
ef_lt_k.params (malformed) ef<k → Err(InvalidParams), not silent truncation

5.6 Pitfalls

  • Stop-condition off-by-one: cd >= worst vs cd > worst shifts recall + dist-comps. Use strict cd > results.peek(). Debug: log expansions vs recall on ef_sweep.
  • VisitedSet gen overflow: after 2^32 queries gen wraps; stale marks → false "visited" → silent recall drop. Cause: skipped wrap-reset. Detect: assert gen!=0 after increment; force full clear on wrap.
  • Result heap uncapped: forgetting if len>ef pop() → O(N) memory, wrong stop. Symptom: latency grows with graph size.
  • HNSW upper layers with ef>1: wastes comps; upper layers MUST use ef=1 greedy. Debug: dist-comps/query 2–5× expected.
  • Distance sign for IP/Cosine: heaps assume smaller=closer; raw similarity (not negated) inverts nearest/worst → near-zero recall. Assert Distance came through Metric conversion.
  • Reused ctx without clear(): stale candidates corrupt results. clear() first in every search entry.

5.7 Performance Concerns

  • Dist-comps/query ≈ ef * mean_out_degree * (1+hop_factor) (CostModel, Phase 11) — dominant cost; minimize via ef tuning, not micro-opt.
  • CSR contiguous edges → prefetch &store[next_neighbor] one hop ahead; row-major VectorStore keeps reads sequential.
  • Versioned bitset eliminates per-query vec![false;N] (1M → 125 KB memset removed → O(1)).
  • Heap ops O(log ef); for small ef (≤200) a flat insertion-sorted array beats BinaryHeap on cache — benchmark both.
  • beam_width>1 batches distance calls → SIMD/ILP amortization; sweet spot 4–8.
  • Target single-thread p99 < 1 ms @ ef=64, dim=128, 1M. Multi-reader: Graph/CsrGraph immutable Send+Sync; per-thread SearchContext.

5.8 Edge Cases

  • k > N → return all N sorted. ef < k → Err(InvalidParams).
  • Query == an indexed vector → distance 0, self-ranks #1.
  • Disconnected graph → reaches only EP's component; recall reflects it (no panic).
  • Node with empty NeighborList → expansion no-ops, loop terminates.
  • All-equal distances → stable tie-break by VectorId for determinism.
  • ef=1 → pure greedy descent, may fall below target (allowed, reported).
  • NaN in query → reject at entry (Err(InvalidQuery)); never enters heap ordering.

5.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Infinite loop (cycle + bad visited) iteration cap = N expansions; assert abort → Err(SearchDiverged)
Recall regression after graph swap RecallHarness CI gate block; diff dist-comps + reachability
OOM on huge ef ef capped at N; heaps pre-reserved clamp + warn
Stale visited (gen wrap) gen!=0 invariant full clear
Non-deterministic results tie-break by id; fixed seed repro via tiny_line_graph

5.10 Operational Notes

  • annopt search flags --ef/--k/--beam-width/--report (latency+recall JSON); annopt bench drives RecallHarness across an ef sweep → recall/latency table feeding Phase 11 Autotuner.
  • Per-query metrics emitted: dist-comps, hops-to-converge, ef used, result count.
  • Determinism: search is reproducible given (graph, query, ef, beam_width); record these in bench output.
  • Thread model: N readers, each owns a SearchContext; shared &CsrGraph + &VectorStore; no hot-path locks.
  • Recall@k is graph-quality-dependent: low recall at high ef ⇒ graph problem (Phase 7 diagnostics), not a search bug.

18. Phase 6 — Vamana / DiskANN builder (RobustPrune, alpha, medoid)

6.1 Objectives

  • Implement VamanaBuilder (ann-build): flat single-layer graph, GraphKind::Vamana { r, alpha, l } — no hierarchy, one adjacency level (layers[0]).
  • Entry point = medoid (vector minimizing summed distance to a sample), not random / level-based.
  • Per-node greedy_search collects a visited candidate pool V (every node touched, not just top-k) → fed to robust_prune.
  • robust_prune(p, V, alpha, R): occlusion rule removes v where alpha*d(p*,v) <= d(p,v); cap out-degree at R.
  • Two-pass build: pass 1 alpha=1.0 (RNG-like), pass 2 alpha ∈ [1.2,1.4] (DiskANN long-range edges).
  • Bidirectional edge add p→p* and p*→p; when |out(p*)| > R re-run robust_prune on p*.
  • Target: recall@10 within 0.01 of HNSW at equal mean distance-comps on same corpus; differential parity vs DiskANN.

6.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per [P2] DiskANN/Vamana §3.1 RobustPrune + §3.2 two-pass alpha, [P9] NN-Descent bootstrap context, ADR-07 Vamana pruning, P7-NavigabilityOverRawNearestM, INV-3 connectivity from entry_point, INV-10 prune termination)

Files (section 10): ann-build/src/vamana.rs (VamanaBuilder, two-pass driver, medoid); ann-build/src/prune.rs (robust_prune shared kernel, called by ann-optimize occlusion_prune via PruneStrategy::RobustPrune); reuse ann-search::greedy_search for candidate-pool collection (its VisitedSet = V).

pub struct VamanaBuilder<'s> { store: &'s VectorStore, p: BuildParams, kernel: Box<dyn DistanceKernel> }
impl VamanaBuilder<'_> {
    pub fn build(&mut self) -> Graph;        // GraphKind::Vamana, entry_point = medoid
    fn compute_medoid(&self) -> VectorId;    // sampled argmin Σ d(x,·)
    fn pass(&mut self, g: &mut Graph, order: &[VectorId], alpha: f32);
}
pub fn robust_prune(p: VectorId, candidates: &mut Vec<(VectorId, Distance)>,
                    alpha: f32, r: u32, store: &VectorStore) -> NeighborList;

Per-node step (one pass, node p):

  1. V ← greedy_search(g, vec[p], ep=medoid, ef=L, ctx) visited set ∪ existing out(p); exclude p itself.
  2. out(p) ← robust_prune(p, V, alpha, R).
  3. For each j ∈ out(p): add j→p; if |out(j)| > R → robust_prune(j, out(j)∪{p}, alpha, R).

robust_prune loop:

while !V.empty && |result| < R:
    p* = argmin_{v∈V} d(p, v)                 // closest remaining
    result.push(p*); V.remove(p*)
    V.retain(|v| alpha * d(p*, v) > d(p, v))  // keep only NON-occluded
return NeighborList::from(result)             // sorted asc by d(p,·)
  • Medoid: sample min(n, 10_000) (seeded), exact pairwise on sample, argmin row-sum; store as Graph::entry_point.
  • Init: empty AdjacencyList; visit order = random permutation seeded by BuildParams::seed. Precompute d(p,v) once into the candidate tuple.

6.3 Deliverable

VamanaBuilder::build() → connected flat Graph (GraphKind::Vamana), medoid entry point, max out-degree ≤ R. CLI annopt build --algo vamana --r 64 --l 100 --alpha 1.2 builds and serializes (Phase 2 (de)serialization); searchable via beam_search over the frozen CsrGraph. Integration test builds 100k vectors, measures recall@10 with RecallHarness.

6.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
max out-degree ≤ R for every node (invariant, asserted post-build)
recall@10 (SIFT1M-analog, L=100, R=64, α=1.2) ≥ 0.94
recall gap vs HNSW at equal mean dist-comps ≤ 0.01
medoid determinism identical VectorId across runs at fixed seed
build determinism byte-identical CsrGraph at fixed seed, single thread
two-pass effect pass-2 estimated recall@10 ≥ pass-1 at equal ef
reachability from medoid 100% of nodes (verified Phase 7 diagnostics)

6.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Content / expected
fixtures/vamana/prune_basic.json 8-pt pool + α + R → exact expected NeighborList (hand-computed)
fixtures/vamana/medoid_grid.json 5×5 grid → medoid = center VectorId (deterministic)
fixtures/vamana/twopass_recall.json recall@10 per pass on 10k subset; pass2 ≥ pass1
fixtures/vamana/diskann_diff/ DiskANN-built edge sets; median per-node Jaccard(edges) ≥ 0.85
malformed: R=0 Err(InvalidParams) (degree ≥ 1)
malformed: alpha < 1.0 Err(AlphaBelowOne)
malformed: empty pool V robust_prune returns empty NeighborList, no panic

6.6 Pitfalls

  • Occlusion direction inverted (<= vs >, or wrong side d(p,v) vs d(p*,v)): collapses to nearest-R, recall craters. Root: retain keeps NON-occluded alpha*d(p*,v) > d(p,v). Debug: dump kept-vs-pruned for prune_basic.json.
  • Self-loop: p in its own V → edge p→p. Filter p before prune; assert no self-edges.
  • Pass-2 seeded from empty graph: pass 2 must greedy_search the pass-1 graph, else long-range edges never form; symptom = α has zero effect on recall.
  • Back-edge re-prune skipped: |out(j)| grows unbounded > R; degree invariant violated. Always re-prune j when over R.
  • Medoid full O(n²): 1M vectors → 10¹² dist-comps. Must sample. Symptom: build hangs in compute_medoid.
  • IP/Cosine sign: medoid argmin and all comparisons use Distance (negated-sim), not raw similarity — else picks the anti-medoid.

6.7 Performance Concerns

  • Build cost ≈ n * L * mean_degree dist-comps; SIFT1M @ L=100,R=64 ≈ 5–9 min single-thread.
  • robust_prune O(|V|²) in retain rescans; bound |V| ≤ L (~64–200). Precompute d(p,v) once, never recompute on retain.
  • Two passes double build cost — expose --passes 1 for the recall/build-time tradeoff.
  • Parallel node sweep: per-node NeighborList lock (back-edges contend) or sharded locks; deterministic mode forces single-thread.
  • Reuse one SearchContext per worker (VisitedSet O(1) reset) — no per-node allocation.

6.8 Edge Cases

  • n=1: medoid = node 0, empty adjacency, search returns it.
  • n ≤ R+1: every node links to all others (prune leaves full set ≤ R).
  • Duplicate vectors (d=0): occlusion alpha*0 <= 0 prunes ties aggressively → keep lowest VectorId for determinism.
  • alpha=1.0 exactly: pure RNG rule; pass-1 must use it.
  • L < R: too few candidates to fill R → under-degree; warn and clamp L ≥ R.
  • Disconnected component (rare isolated cluster): deferred to Phase 9 connect_components; Phase 6 asserts ≤ R only, not full reachability.

6.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Mitigation
recall far below HNSW RecallHarness sample check occlusion sign; confirm two-pass ran
degree > R post-build assert / Phase 7 histogram fix back-edge re-prune
build non-deterministic hash CsrGraph twice plumb seed to permutation + medoid sample; force single-thread
medoid drift medoid_grid fixture fix sample seed; add VectorId tie-break
robust_prune panic on empty V fuzz target early-return empty NeighborList

6.10 Operational Notes

  • CLI: annopt build --algo vamana --r <R> --l <L> --alpha <α> --passes <1|2> --seed <s>.
  • Defaults: R=64, L=100, alpha=1.2, passes=2 (DiskANN-typical). Larger R → higher recall, more memory + dist-comps.
  • Build log: medoid VectorId, per-pass elapsed, final mean/max out-degree, recall@10 on held-out sample.
  • Vamana graph feeds Phase 8 occlusion_prune (same robust_prune via PruneStrategy::RobustPrune { alpha }) and Phase 12 on-disk DiskANN beam-search + re-rank path.
  • Tag graph metadata (R, alpha, passes, seed) for reproducibility and Autotuner (Phase 11) grid context.

19. Phase 7 — Graph diagnostics (degree, connectivity, navigability, recall estimate)

The optimizer's measurement substrate. Read-only analysis of a built Graph/CsrGraph: degree distributions, connectivity (components, reachability from entry_point), a sampled navigability proxy, and an estimated recall@10 vs exact KNN. Every later optimize pass (Phases 8–11) is gated on these numbers (the OPTIMIZER GUARANTEE: no silent recall regression). NON-GOAL: mutating the graph — that is Phase 8+.

7.1 Objectives

  • GraphDiagnostics populated for any Graph (HNSW base layer or Vamana flat) or CsrGraph.
  • Out-degree: mean_out_degree, out_degree_hist[64] (bucket = min(degree,63)).
  • In-degree via edge transpose: min_in_degree, orphan_count (in-degree==0, excluding entry_point).
  • Connectivity: connected_components (weak, undirected union), unreachable_from_ep (directed BFS from entry_point).
  • navigability_score: sampled mean hops-to-convergence proxy → normalized [0,1].
  • estimated_recall_at_10: S sampled queries vs RecallHarness exact KNN at fixed ef.
  • annopt diagnose emits human-readable report; --json machine-readable. Deterministic given seed.

7.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per [P8] NGT/ONNG in/out-degree optimization, [P4] NSG connectivity guarantee, P1-RecallMeasuredNeverAssumed, ADR-12 optimizer safety gate, INV-3 connectivity from entry_point, INV-6 optimizer guarantee)

Files (all in ann-optimize):

File Contents
ann-optimize/src/diagnostics.rs GraphDiagnostics, compute_diagnostics, orchestration
ann-optimize/src/degree.rs out_degree_stats, transpose_in_degree
ann-optimize/src/connectivity.rs weak_components (union-find), reachable_from_ep (BFS)
ann-optimize/src/navigability.rs sampled_navigability, estimate_recall
ann-optimize/src/report.rs DiagnosticReport, Display + to_json
ann-cli/src/cmd/diagnose.rs annopt diagnose wiring

Signatures:

pub fn compute_diagnostics(g:&Graph, store:&VectorStore, h:&RecallHarness,
                           cfg:&DiagConfig) -> GraphDiagnostics;
pub struct DiagConfig { pub sample:u32, pub ef:u32, pub k:u32, pub seed:u64 }
fn transpose_in_degree(g:&Graph) -> Vec<u32>;      // one pass over all edges → in[v]++
fn weak_components(g:&Graph) -> (u32, Vec<u32>);   // union-find, edges as undirected
fn reachable_from_ep(g:&Graph) -> (u32, BitVec);   // directed BFS; reachable count + mask
  • Degree pass: iterate AdjacencyList.neighbors[i].len() → mean + hist. CSR: offsets[i+1]-offsets[i].
  • In-degree: zeroed Vec<u32>[n]; for each u, each v in out(u): in[v]+=1. orphan_count = count(in[v]==0, v!=ep); min_in_degree = min over v!=ep.
  • Weak components: union-find (path-compression + rank) over both directions of every edge; connected_components = distinct roots.
  • Reachability: directed BFS from entry_point over out(); unreachable_from_ep = n - reachable.
  • Navigability: for S sampled nodes, run greedy_search toward node's own vector (self-query) at fixed ef, count expansion hops to convergence; navigability_score = 1 - clamp(mean_hops / hop_ceiling, 0, 1), hop_ceiling = c·log2(n).
  • Recall estimate: S query vectors from harness held-out set; greedy_search(g,q,ep,ef) top-k vs exact; estimated_recall_at_10 = mean(|found∩truth|/k) at k=10.
  • HNSW: diagnostics on layers[0] (base) only; upper-layer health is a separate counter (upper_layer_orphans, reported, not in locked struct).

7.3 Deliverable

annopt diagnose --graph g.bin --vectors v.fvecs [--gt gt.ivecs] [--sample 1000 --ef 100 --json] prints a GraphDiagnostics report flagging orphans, components >1, and the recall estimate; exit 0. Library entry compute_diagnostics callable from optimizer + tests.

7.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • 1M-node graph: full diagnostics (excl. recall sample) < 500 ms single-thread; in-degree transpose < 100 ms.
  • Recall estimate within ±0.01 of full-set recall@10 at S=1000 (vs exact over all queries).
  • connected_components/unreachable_from_ep exact (cross-checked vs independent reference BFS in tests).
  • Deterministic: identical report bytes for fixed (graph, seed, sample, ef) across runs/platforms.
  • Graph with injected orphan/disconnected component correctly flagged (counts match injection).

7.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
diag_clean_1k.bin orphan_count=0, connected_components=1, unreachable_from_ep=0, recall@10 ≥ 0.95
diag_orphan_3.bin orphan_count=3, min_in_degree=0
diag_2comp.bin connected_components=2, unreachable_from_ep=size of non-EP component
diag_degree_hist.golden.json exact out_degree_hist[64] + mean_out_degree
diag_recall.golden estimated_recall_at_10 ± tol vs diag_recall_full.golden
Malformed diag_ep_oob.bin entry_point >= n → DiagError::EntryPointOutOfRange (no panic)
Malformed diag_self_loop.bin self-edge u->u excluded from in-degree; reported as self_loops count
Malformed diag_dup_edge.bin duplicate u->v counted once for connectivity; flagged duplicate_edges

7.6 Pitfalls

  • EP counted as orphan: entry_point often has in-degree 0 by construction → exclude EP from orphan_count/min_in_degree. Debug: assert orphan set excludes entry_point.
  • Weak vs strong/reachable confusion: connected_components is undirected (weak); reachability is directed. Graph can be 1 weak component yet unreachable_from_ep>0. Reporting only one hides the failure — emit both.
  • Self-query recall=1.0 trap: self-query trivially returns the start node, inflating recall. Use held-out queries for estimated_recall_at_10; self-query only for hop counting, excluding the seed node from results.
  • Sampling bias toward hubs: uniform-over-edges sample over-represents reachable core, masks tail orphans. Sample uniformly over VectorId range, not edges. Debug: compare sampled vs full orphan_count on fixtures.
  • Stale neighbor IDs after deletes: scatter into wrong index corrupts in-degree counts. Validate every neighbor < n before transpose.
  • hop_ceiling miscalibration: navigability_score saturates to 0/1 if c·log2(n) ceiling wrong; pin c per fixture, surface raw mean_hops alongside normalized score.

7.7 Performance Concerns

  • Transpose: O(E) single pass; scatter writes are cache-miss heavy — pre-size Vec<u32> to n, no resizing. ~R·n ops.
  • BFS + union-find: O(V+E); union-find near-O(α(n)) with path-compression + rank.
  • Recall/navigability sample dominates wall time (S × ef·R dist-comps); parallelize across S with rayon over read-only Graph; one SearchContext per worker (versioned VisitedSet, O(1) reset).
  • out_degree_hist stack-allocated [u32;64], no heap.

7.8 Edge Cases

  • n==1: single node = EP; 1 component, 0 orphans, recall n/a.
  • No ground truth: skip estimated_recall_at_10 (None/-1), still emit structural diagnostics.
  • All-isolated (no edges): connected_components=n, unreachable_from_ep=n-1, mean_out_degree=0.
  • Vamana flat: layers.len()==1, no upper-layer path taken.
  • sample > n: clamp to n, sample without replacement.
  • EP unreachable-from-rest is expected (directed) — only flag nodes EP cannot reach.

7.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
entry_point out of range bounds check pre-BFS DiagError::EntryPointOutOfRange, abort
Neighbor ID ≥ n (corrupt graph) validate during degree pass DiagError::EdgeOutOfRange{u,v}
Recall estimate > 1.0 post-check clamp + assert indicates dup truth IDs — fail loud in debug
Diverging component count on rebuild golden mismatch regression gate fails CI
NaN distance in navigability kernel returns non-finite DiagError::NonFiniteDistance, point to offending VectorId

7.10 Operational Notes

  • annopt diagnose is pre-flight for every optimize/tune run; CI gate: orphan_count==0 && unreachable_from_ep==0 && estimated_recall_at_10>=target else non-zero exit.
  • --json schema versioned (diag_schema_v1) for dashboards; human report renders ASCII bars for out_degree_hist.
  • Diagnostics are pure reads → safe concurrent with multi-reader search; never mutate Graph.
  • Log seed, sample, ef in report header so flagged results reproduce.
  • Baseline snapshot stored post-build; Phase 8+ passes diff against it to prove the OPTIMIZER GUARANTEE (recall non-regression).

20. Phase 8 — Optimizer pruning passes (occlusion prune, rebalance, reverse edges)

Core of ann-optimize: rewrite an already-built Graph (HNSW or Vamana) to lower mean out-degree and/or raise recall-per-distance-comp, under the optimizer guarantee (no recall@k regression at fixed ef; degree ≤ R; reachability preserved). Three composable passes; each measures sampled recall before/after and rolls back on regression.

8.1 Objectives

  • occlusion_prune(g, strat, store) — global edge re-selection sweep; drop occluded/redundant edges via PruneStrategy::{RobustPrune, HeuristicSelect, RelativeNeighborhood}.
  • degree_rebalance(g, r) — cap every over-degree node to R using the select-neighbors heuristic (keep diverse, navigable subset; not naive nearest-R truncation).
  • reverse_edge_repair(g, min_in_degree) — make orphan / low-in-degree nodes discoverable by adding reverse edges from their out-neighbors.
  • Enforce guarantee: re-run GraphDiagnostics sampled recall@k at fixed ef; reject/rollback any pass regressing recall beyond tolerance.
  • Deliverable: optimized graph with measurable improvement on mean_out_degree and/or recall/dist-comp, proven by before/after diagnostic diff.

8.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per [P10] Toussaint RNG pruning criterion, [P2] RobustPrune alpha relaxation, [P8] NGT degree balance, P3-OptimizationNeverSilentlyRegressesRecall, P7-NavigabilityOverRawNearestM, ADR-12 optimizer safety gate, INV-6 optimizer guarantee)

Files under ann-optimize/src/:

File Contents
prune.rs occlusion_prune, PruneStrategy, RNG predicate, occlusion test
rebalance.rs degree_rebalance (heuristic cap to R)
reverse.rs reverse_edge_repair (in-degree census + reverse insertion)
guard.rs OptimizerGuard (snapshot / measure / rollback wrapper)

Algorithms:

  • occlusion_prune: per node p (parallel over base layer; HNSW: all layers): candidate pool V = current out-neighbors + optionally their 1-hop (extend), sorted asc by d(p,·); re-select via strat → new NeighborList capped at max_degree (R / M_max0 by layer).
    • RobustPrune{alpha} → reuse ann-build::robust_prune(p, &mut V, alpha, R, store); occlusion test alpha*d(p*,v) <= d(p,v) removes v.
    • RelativeNeighborhood → RNG predicate: keep p→v iff no selected r with d(p,r)<d(p,v) && d(r,v)<d(p,v) (α=1 special case).
    • HeuristicSelect{keep_pruned} → ann-build::select_neighbors_heuristic(&V, M, extend=true, keep_pruned).
  • degree_rebalance: scan nodes; if out_degree(p) > r, pool = current (dist-sorted) neighbors, apply heuristic select to R, write back. No new distance comps (dists cached in NeighborList).
  • reverse_edge_repair: build in_deg[VectorId] via one pass over all NeighborLists; for each p with in_deg[p] < min_in_degree (orphan = 0), for each out-neighbor q of p add edge q→p if absent and out_degree(q) < max_degree; if q full, evict q's farthest only when d(q,p) closer (preserve navigability). Recompute affected in_deg.
  • OptimizerGuard: snapshot() = copy-on-write per touched node set; apply(pass); measure() via GraphDiagnostics::estimated_recall_at_10 on fixed sample S; if recall_after < recall_before - tol → rollback().

Signatures (locked):
