pub fn occlusion_prune(g: &mut Graph, strat: PruneStrategy, store: &VectorStore);
pub fn degree_rebalance(g: &mut Graph, r: u32);
pub fn reverse_edge_repair(g: &mut Graph, min_in_degree: u32);

8.3 Deliverable

annopt optimize --in raw.graph --out opt.graph --pass prune,rebalance,reverse --alpha 1.2 produces an optimized Graph + before/after GraphDiagnostics JSON. Library API: the three passes + OptimizerGuard. Each pass idempotent on second run (within tolerance).

8.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • 100k-vector SIFT-analog HNSW: occlusion_prune lowers mean_out_degree ≥ 10% with estimated_recall_at_10 drop ≤ 0.5%, or improves recall/dist-comp ratio ≥ 5%.
  • degree_rebalance(g, R) → post-condition max(out_degree) ≤ R all nodes (assert).
  • reverse_edge_repair(g, 1) → orphan_count == 0 and min_in_degree ≥ 1.
  • Guarantee: no run reports recall_after < recall_before - 0.005; synthetic regressing pass is rolled back (test asserts graph bytes unchanged).
  • All passes deterministic given seed and fixed sample S.

8.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input Expected
prune_rng_8node.json 8-node 2D dense graph RNG removes 2 occluded edges; golden surviving edge set
robustprune_alpha12.json 50-node, α=1.2 matches DiskANN reference adjacency (golden)
rebalance_overdeg.json node out_degree=R+5 post out_degree==R, kept = heuristic-selected ids
reverse_orphan.json 1 orphan (in_deg=0) reverse edges added; orphan_count 1→0
prune_empty_pool.json (malformed) node, 0 neighbors no-op, no panic
reverse_full_neighbors.json (malformed) all out-neighbors at max_degree eviction only if closer; else logged skip

8.6 Pitfalls

  • Self-loop / dup edges after reverse repair — root: adding q→p already present or p==q. Debug: assert no self-loops + dedup NeighborList post-pass; membership check before insert.
  • Stale cached distances — root: NeighborList.dists reused after vectors edited → heuristic keeps wrong edges. Debug: recompute one neighbor, compare; never reuse dists across vector edits.
  • Asymmetric occlusion → disconnection — pruning p→q may leave q unreachable if its only in-edge; recall drops. Debug: reachability before/after; reverse_edge_repair must run after prune (full repair is Phase 9).
  • Heuristic with negated-similarity Distance — root: IP/Cosine stored as -sim; sign flip in d(e,r) keeps wrong edges. Debug: unit-test RNG on Cosine fixture.
  • Rebalance truncating to nearest-R (not heuristic) — destroys long-range navigability; recall collapses at high dim. Debug: compare degree-hist + recall vs heuristic variant.
  • Non-deterministic parallel writes — two threads editing same q→p reverse edge race; per-node lock or partition by node id.

8.7 Performance Concerns

  • occlusion_prune dominant cost = candidate-pool distance comps; with extend (1-hop) pool ~ R² → O(N·R²) comps. Cache d(p,·) from build; budget via DistanceCounter.
  • Parallelize over nodes with rayon; partition node-id space to avoid reverse-edge write races. Target: optimize 1M-vector graph < 2× build time.
  • degree_rebalance O(N·R log R), no new distance comps → < 1s for 1M nodes.
  • reverse_edge_repair: in-degree census O(N·R) single pass; insertion O(orphans·R).

8.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty / single-node graph → all passes no-op.
  • All nodes already ≤ R → rebalance no-op (assert unchanged bytes).
  • Fully connected small graph → RNG may prune to sparse navigable core; verify still searchable.
  • Duplicate vectors (d=0) → alpha*0 <= d(p,v) always true → over-prunes; tie-break by VectorId, keep one.
  • HNSW upper layers (few nodes) → use M_max not M_max0; never orphan the entry point (skip EP in pruning that would zero its in-degree).
  • min_in_degree larger than achievable (sparse graph) → cap at feasible; warn.

8.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Recall regression OptimizerGuard::measure vs baseline rollback snapshot, return original graph
Degree invariant broken post-pass assert out_degree ≤ R abort, dump offending node
Entry point orphaned reachability check reselect_entry_point (Phase 9) or block pass
OOM on snapshot of 1M graph copy-on-write per touched node, not full clone snapshot only touched node set
Distance budget exceeded DistanceCounter ceiling abort pass, report partial

8.10 Operational Notes

  • annopt optimize logs per-pass diagnostic delta (mean_out_degree, recall@10, dist-comps) as a table; --dry-run measures without writing.
  • Recommended order: occlusion_prune → degree_rebalance → reverse_edge_repair (prune first, then guarantee discoverability). connect_components (Phase 9) runs after.
  • Tolerance tol=0.005 recall via --recall-tol; sample size S=1000 queries default (seeded).
  • Persist before/after GraphDiagnostics to --report opt.json for regression gates (Phase 13).
  • Idempotent: re-running with same params yields byte-identical graph (deterministic seed + fixed S).

21. Phase 9 — Connectivity repair + compaction + entry-point reselection

Closes the optimizer guarantee: Phase 8 pruning may sever edges, so this phase forces connected_components == 1, unreachable_from_ep == 0, reselects the entry point at the graph medoid/highest-centrality node, and renumbers nodes for CSR cache locality. Work lives in ann-optimize on the mutable Graph build form; CsrGraph re-emission happens at compaction.

9.1 Objectives

  • connect_components(g, store) → bridge every unreachable subgraph to the EP component; BFS-reachable set covers all len nodes.
  • nsg_spanning_repair(g, store) → NSG-style DFS spanning tree rooted at navigating node; hard backstop guaranteeing reachability.
  • reselect_entry_point(g, store) -> VectorId → medoid (min mean-dist to sample) / max-centrality node; updates g.entry_point.
  • Compaction → renumber VectorIds in BFS order from EP for sequential CSR edges[] access; emit CsrGraph + permutation.
  • Verify monotonic-search-path on sample S: greedy hop sequence strictly decreasing in d(·,q) to convergence.

9.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per [P4] NSG §4.2 DFS spanning tree + navigating node, P6-EveryNodeReachableFromEntryPoint, P3-OptimizationNeverSilentlyRegressesRecall, ADR-12 optimizer safety gate, INV-3 connectivity, INV-6 optimizer guarantee)

Files (ann-optimize): connect.rs (connect_components, nsg_spanning_repair), entry.rs (reselect_entry_point, centrality), compact.rs (renumber + CSR emit), monotonic.rs (path check).

// connect.rs
pub fn connect_components(g: &mut Graph, store: &VectorStore);
pub fn nsg_spanning_repair(g: &mut Graph, store: &VectorStore);
fn reachable_from(g: &Graph, ep: VectorId) -> BitVec;            // BFS over base layer layers[0]
fn label_components(g: &Graph) -> (Vec<u32>, u32);              // union-find on undirected view -> (comp_id, count)
fn component_medoid(ids: &[VectorId], store: &VectorStore) -> VectorId; // sampled min-mean-dist
// entry.rs
pub fn reselect_entry_point(g: &Graph, store: &VectorStore) -> VectorId;
fn centrality_score(g: &Graph, v: VectorId, sample: &[VectorId], store: &VectorStore) -> f32; // w_in*in_deg + w_close*closeness
// compact.rs
pub fn compact(g: &Graph, store: &VectorStore) -> (CsrGraph, Vec<u32>);   // (csr, old->new perm)
  • connect_components: BFS from EP over layers[0].adjacency → reach bitset. If all set, no-op. Else union-find over directed-as-undirected edges → labels. For each non-EP component C: m = component_medoid(C); restricted greedy-search m's vector against the reachable set → nearest reachable t; add directed m->t and t->m via select_neighbors_heuristic, marking both pinned (exempt from R-eviction). Re-run BFS until single component.
  • nsg_spanning_repair: backstop after connect_components if any node still unreachable (R-eviction may have undone a bridge). DFS from navigating node (the reselected EP); on visiting unreached v, forcibly add parent->v (pinned, over-degree tolerated). Spanning tree ⊆ graph ⇒ 100% reachability; final degree_rebalance re-call respects pins.
  • reselect_entry_point: sample √len nodes; score = w_in·norm_in_degree + w_close·(1/mean_dist_to_sample); argmax. Vamana path prefers recomputed medoid. Run before bridging so bridges target the new larger EP component.
  • compact: BFS-order traversal from EP assigns new ids 0..len; perm old->new; remap all NeighborList.ids; re-sort each list asc by dist (ids changed, dists unchanged); emit CsrGraph { offsets, edges, entry_point: 0 } (EP becomes id 0). Strip self-loops + dangling edges.
  • monotonic check: replay greedy_search hop trace per sampled query; assert each accepted move strictly decreases best Distance; count violations.

9.3 Deliverable

annopt optimize --repair <graph> -o <out> runs connect_components → nsg_spanning_repair → reselect_entry_point → compact → final degree_rebalance, emits repaired CsrGraph + report (GraphDiagnostics before/after, bridge edges, perm checksum, monotonic violation rate on S).

9.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
connected_components == 1
unreachable_from_ep == 0 (all len nodes BFS-reachable)
max out-degree post-repair ≤ R (after final rebalance, pins respected)
recall@10 at fixed ef vs pre-repair ≥ pre-repair (optimizer guarantee; no regression)
monotonic-path violations on S=1000 ≤ 1% of queries
EP medoid mean-dist ≤ pre-repair EP mean-dist
bridge edges added ≤ components_before × 2
repair wall time on 1M graph ≤ 0.5× original build time

9.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Input Expected
two_islands.graph 2 disjoint 500-node clusters comps 2→1; ≥1 medoid↔reachable bridge; reach=1000
orphan_chain.graph EP + isolated 10-chain nsg_spanning_repair reattaches all 10
already_connected.graph healthy HNSW 10k no-op; 0 bridges; EP unchanged-or-improved
star_low_centrality_ep.graph EP is a leaf reselect moves EP to hub; mean-dist drops
compact_perm.golden post-compact perm bijective 0..len; EP→0; CSR offsets monotonic
Malformed dangling_edge.graph edge → id ≥ len reject InvalidNeighbor, no panic
Malformed self_loop.graph v→v edge stripped during compact; logged

9.6 Pitfalls

  • R-eviction undoes bridges: m->t evicts the only return path → component re-splits. Root: degree cap honored before reachability. Fix: pinned bridges exempt from eviction until final rebalance; debug via per-bridge post-BFS recheck.
  • Bridge to wrong component: restricted greedy from m lands in another unreachable component, still disconnected. Root: searching full graph not reach-masked. Fix: mask candidates by reach; assert reach[t].
  • Compaction breaks dist-sort: remap ids without re-sort → NeighborList unsorted → search early-termination wrong. Root: dists stay, ids change. Fix: re-sort by dist post-remap; covered by compact_perm.golden.
  • Medoid O(n²): exact medoid over full component quadratic on 1M. Root: all-pairs. Fix: √|C| sampled anchors; timing histogram.
  • Centrality dominated by in-degree noise: high-in-degree hub far from data center → longer paths. Root: weight imbalance. Fix: validate via mean-hops drop; tune w_in/w_close.
  • nsg over-degree leak: forced spanning edge pruned by final rebalance → unreachable again. Fix: rebalance treats spanning/bridge edges as pinned.

9.7 Performance Concerns

  • BFS reachability: O(V+E)/pass; ≤ comps passes → O(comps·(V+E)); comps typically small.
  • Union-find (path compression + rank): ~O(α(n))/edge; full label pass O(E·α).
  • Medoid sampling: √|C|·√|C| = O(|C|) distance comps/component, charged to DistanceCounter.
  • Compaction: single BFS O(V+E) + remap O(E); post-renumber cache-miss reduction ≥20% L2 misses on base-layer scan (perf-counter measured).
  • Whole repair pass target < 0.5× original build time on 1M.

9.8 Edge Cases

  • len==1: EP=0, comps=1, no-op.
  • Fully connected: connect_components no-op; compact still renumbers (EP→0).
  • Degree-0 graph (all orphan except EP): nsg builds near-linear tree; warn navigability_score collapse.
  • Duplicate vectors (dist=0): medoid ties broken by lowest VectorId (deterministic).
  • EP pre-corrupted into tiny component: reselect runs first ⇒ bridges target the new EP component.
  • len==0: return empty CsrGraph; no panic.

9.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Still multi-component post-repair final BFS asserts reach==len invoke nsg_spanning_repair backstop; else hard error + dump component sizes
Recall regressed vs pre-repair RecallHarness sample compare reject output, keep pre-repair graph, log offending bridges
Perm not bijective checksum + new-id bitset abort compact; corruption flag
Monotonic violations > 1% monotonic.rs counter warn (non-fatal); mark low-quality for autotuner
Degree > R after repair diagnostics max-degree re-run degree_rebalance with pinned-edge exemption

9.10 Operational Notes

  • annopt diagnose reports comps/unreachable so --repair runs only when needed (idempotent: re-run on healthy graph ≈ no-op).
  • Repair is offline/batch (mutates build-form Graph, not live mmap CsrGraph); swap atomically via Phase 12 save_graph.
  • Structured log: {comps_before, bridges_added, ep_old, ep_new, ep_mean_dist_delta, monotonic_viol_pct}.
  • Determinism: fixed RNG seed for medoid sampling ⇒ identical bridges/perm across runs (required for differential tests vs NSG).
  • Pinned-edge set persisted in repair report for audit; cleared after final rebalance.

22. Phase 10 — Incremental maintenance (insert, delete, re-optimize)

Online mutation of an already-optimized Graph/CsrGraph without full rebuild. Inserts splice new nodes via search-layer + select_neighbors_heuristic + bidirectional link + local re-prune; deletes are Tombstone-marked in a DeleteSet with lazy relink (bridge in-neighbors → out-neighbors); local re-optimization confines occlusion_prune/reverse_edge_repair to the affected neighborhood; periodic consolidation physically evicts tombstones and rebuilds CSR. Single node, single-writer / multi-reader.

10.1 Objectives

  • Insert into optimized Graph (HNSW or Vamana) at amortized cost ≈ one build-time insert; recall@k drift ≤ 2 pts vs full rebuild after up to 20% churn.
  • Delete = O(1) tombstone mark + bounded lazy relink; never orphans a live node, never returns a tombstoned VectorId from search.
  • Local re-optimize: re-run pruning only over affected_set (insert/delete neighborhoods ∪ 1-hop), not the whole graph.
  • Consolidation: physically drop tombstoned nodes, remap VectorIds, rebuild CsrGraph; atomic + crash-recoverable.

10.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per [P6] FreshDiskANN streaming insert/delete + consolidation, P11-TombstonesNotInPlaceDeletes, ADR-16 delete semantics, P3-OptimizationNeverSilentlyRegressesRecall, INV-2 edges point to live nodes, INV-3 connectivity)

Home: ann-optimize (maintenance ops) + ann-store (Tombstone, DeleteSet, consolidation); inserts reuse ann-build (search-layer, heuristics) and ann-search (SearchContext).

// ann-optimize
pub fn insert(g: &mut Graph, id: VectorId, v: &[f32], store: &VectorStore, bp: &BuildParams) -> InsertReport;
pub fn delete(g: &mut Graph, dels: &mut DeleteSet, id: VectorId, store: &VectorStore) -> DeleteReport;
pub fn local_reoptimize(g: &mut Graph, affected: &[VectorId], strat: PruneStrategy, store: &VectorStore);
pub struct InsertReport { affected: SmallVec<[VectorId; 32]>, new_level: u8, edges_added: u32 }
pub struct DeleteReport { relinked_pairs: u32, affected: SmallVec<[VectorId; 32]> }
// ann-store
pub fn consolidate(g: &Graph, dels: &DeleteSet, store: &VectorStore) -> (CsrGraph, VectorStore, IdRemap);
pub struct IdRemap { old_to_new: Vec<Option<VectorId>> }
  • Insert (HNSW): assign level L = floor(-ln(U)*ml), ml = 1/ln(M); greedy descend ef=1 from entry_point to level L+1; from L..=0 run search-layer(ef_construction), select_neighbors_heuristic(cands, M, extend, keep_pruned), add bidirectional edges; any neighbor whose out-degree > M_max/M_max0 is re-pruned via the heuristic (Phase 4). If L > entry_point level → update entry_point.
  • Insert (Vamana): greedy_search(L) → visited candidate pool → robust_prune(id, pool, alpha, r, store); add reverse edges; re-prune any neighbor exceeding r. Single flat layer.
  • Delete (lazy): set dels.bits[id]=1 (Tombstone); search filters tombstoned ids on result-heap push but keeps them traversable for navigation. Lazy relink: for each in-neighbor u of tombstone t, splice u → liveout(t) (out-neighbors of t minus tombstoned), then re-prune u to M_max. Remove edges into t.
  • Local re-optimize: affected = InsertReport.affected ∪ DeleteReport.affected, expanded 1-hop; run occlusion_prune(strat) + reverse_edge_repair(min_in_degree) restricted to that vertex subset; verify OPTIMIZER GUARANTEE on a sample over affected.
  • Consolidation: build IdRemap packing live nodes → dense [0..n_live); rewrite VectorStore rows + adjacency through remap; rebuild distance-sorted NeighborLists; emit immutable CsrGraph (offsets/edges/entry_point). Trigger when tombstone_ratio > 0.2.

10.3 Deliverable

annopt optimize --insert <vecs> | --delete <ids> | --consolidate plus the three library APIs. After arbitrary insert/delete sequences the graph stays a valid navigable graph (all live nodes reachable from entry_point, out-degree ≤ R/M_max, no tombstone returned), with recall@k within a bounded delta of a from-scratch rebuild on the same final live set.

10.4 Acceptance Criteria

  • Insert 10k into a 100k optimized graph: recall@10 within 2.0 pts of full rebuild on the 110k set; mean insert ≤ 1.3× build-time per-node distance comps.
  • Delete 20%, then search: 0 tombstoned ids returned; 0 orphaned live nodes (reachability from entry_point covers all live).
  • Post-consolidate: connected_components == 1, orphan_count == 0, max out-degree ≤ R, IdRemap bijective over the live set.
  • Local re-optimize touches ≤ |affected|·(1+mean_out_degree) nodes; recall@k on the affected sample never decreases (OPTIMIZER GUARANTEE).

10.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Input Expected
incr_insert_recall.json 100k base + 10k inserts recall@10 ≥ rebuild − 0.02
delete_no_orphan.json delete 20% ids orphan_count==0, all live reachable
lazy_relink_bridge.json delete node in={a,b} out={c,d} a,b gain edges→{c,d}, re-pruned ≤ R
consolidate_remap.json 30% tombstoned dense ids, components==1, no tombstone in CSR
churn_stability.json 50k interleaved ins/del invariants hold every 5k ops
insert_dup_id.json (malformed) re-insert live id Err(DuplicateId)
delete_absent.json (malformed) delete tombstoned/absent id no-op, relinked_pairs==0

10.6 Pitfalls

  • Tombstone leaked in results: filter applied at traversal but forgotten on result-heap push (or vice-versa). Debug: assert !dels.bits[r.id] on every returned SearchResult.
  • Orphan via delete cascade: bridging in→out where out(t) itself contains tombstoned nodes connects in-neighbors to dead nodes → orphan. Root cause: relink to raw out(t). Fix: relink only to live out-neighbors; re-check reachability.
  • Degree blow-up under insert churn: bidirectional add without re-prune lets in-degree grow unbounded → search slowdown. Detect via out_degree_hist tail; enforce re-prune to M_max after every link.
  • Entry-point deleted: tombstoning entry_point strands all queries. Must call reselect_entry_point on EP delete before search resumes.
  • Stale CsrGraph: optimized CSR is immutable; mutations apply to the Graph build form only. Searching the old mmap'd CSR returns pre-delete results. Invariant: invalidate + regenerate CSR on consolidate; version-stamp.
  • Non-deterministic level RNG on re-insert: a shared global RNG breaks differential reproducibility. Seed per-insert from BuildParams.seed ⊕ id.0.

10.7 Performance Concerns

  • Insert hot path = search-layer(ef_construction), dominated by distance comps; reuse a thread-local SearchContext (versioned VisitedSet, O(1) reset) to avoid per-insert allocation.
  • Delete is O(1) mark; lazy relink amortized over in-degree (≤ R) of the tombstone — bounded.
  • Consolidation is O(n_live · mean_out_degree) single pass; batch tombstones to ratio > 0.2 before triggering to amortize the CSR rebuild.
  • Multi-reader search proceeds on a stable snapshot during single-writer mutation; copy-on-write the NeighborList under edit to avoid read tearing.

10.8 Edge Cases

  • Insert into empty / single-node graph → new node becomes entry_point, no neighbors.
  • Delete the last live node → graph empty, search returns [], entry_point set to sentinel.
  • Insert id == live id → Err(DuplicateId); insert id == tombstoned id → revive (clear bit) + re-link.
  • Self-loop candidate (id in its own neighbor pool) → excluded before linking.
  • All neighbors of a node tombstoned → node becomes orphan candidate; caught by reverse_edge_repair.
  • Consolidate with 0 tombstones → identity IdRemap, CSR rebuilt unchanged (idempotent).

10.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Recall regression after churn sample recall@k drop > 2 pts full optimize pass or rebuild
Orphan introduced connect_components finds > 1 component connect_components + reverse_edge_repair
Crash mid-consolidate partial CSR file / version mismatch write temp + atomic rename; fall back to pre-consolidate Graph + DeleteSet
Degree overflow NeighborList cap exceeded re-prune offending node, log
EP tombstoned undetected search returns 0 results reselect_entry_point; assert EP live pre-search

10.10 Operational Notes

  • Metrics: tombstone_ratio, inserts/sec, mean relinked_pairs, post-op GraphDiagnostics (mean_out_degree, orphan_count, connected_components).
  • Policy: auto-consolidate at tombstone_ratio > 0.2; auto local_reoptimize when affected-sample recall drops > 1 pt.
  • Single-writer lock for mutations; readers lock-free on the snapshot. Persist DeleteSet beside the graph; replay on load.
  • annopt diagnose after a churn batch confirms invariants before promoting a fresh CsrGraph to the search path.

23. Phase 11 — Cost model and the recall/latency autotuner

11.1 Objectives

  • Build CostModel (ann-optimize): predict mean distance-computations/query as ef * mean_out_degree * (1 + hop_factor), calibrated from GraphDiagnostics + sampled search runs.
  • Build Autotuner: bisection over ef → smallest ef meeting a RecallTarget; grid sweep over build params (alpha, R, L) for Vamana, (M, ef_construction) for HNSW → recall/latency Pareto frontier as Vec<TuneResult>.
  • Wire annopt tune: report frontier + recommended operating point; emit machine-readable JSON + human table.
  • Invariant: tuner never reports an ef/build point whose measured recall (RecallHarness) violates the target; predicted cost is advisory, measured cost is authoritative.

11.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per P2-DistanceComputationIsTheUnitOfCost, P12-TuneToATargetReportThePareto, ADR-13 autotuner search strategy, [P7] ann-benchmarks Pareto methodology, INV-6 optimizer guarantee — measured recall authoritative)

Files: ann-optimize/src/cost.rs (CostModel), ann-optimize/src/autotune.rs (Autotuner), ann-cli/src/cmd/tune.rs.

pub struct CostModel { mean_out_degree: f32, hop_factor: f32, kernel_ns: f32 } // hop_factor from diag navigability
impl CostModel {
    pub fn calibrate(diag: &GraphDiagnostics, samples: &[SampleRun]) -> CostModel; // least-squares fit hop_factor
    pub fn predict_dist_comps(&self, ef: u32) -> f32;     // ef * mean_out_degree * (1+hop_factor)
    pub fn predict_us(&self, ef: u32) -> f32;             // predict_dist_comps * kernel_ns / 1000
}
pub struct Autotuner { target: RecallTarget, sample: SampleSet, ef_lo: u32, ef_hi: u32 }
impl Autotuner {
    pub fn min_ef_for_target(&self, g: &CsrGraph, store: &VectorStore) -> Option<(u32, f32)>; // bisection → (ef, recall)
    pub fn sweep_build(&self, grid: &ParamGrid, store: &VectorStore) -> Vec<TuneResult>;
    pub fn pareto(results: &[TuneResult]) -> Vec<TuneResult>; // non-dominated by (recall↑, dist_comps↓)
}
  • min_ef_for_target: monotone-recall-in-ef assumption → bisect [ef_lo, ef_hi]; at each ef run beam_search over SampleSet, measure recall@k via RecallHarness; return smallest ef with recall >= target.recall. Falls back to linear scan if non-monotone detected.
  • sweep_build: per grid cell rebuild (HnswBuilder/VamanaBuilder) → optimizer passes (Phases 8-9) → min_ef_for_target → record TuneResult { params, recall, mean_dist_comps, p99_us }. Share one immutable VectorStore; parallel over cells (rayon).
  • pareto: sort by mean_dist_comps asc, skyline-sweep keeping strictly increasing recall. Recommended point = lowest mean_dist_comps row with recall >= target.
  • DistanceCounter snapshot per query → mean_dist_comps; warm wall-clock per query → p99_us from sorted latency vector.

11.3 Deliverable

annopt tune --graph g.bin --target recall@10>=0.95 --grid alpha=1.0,1.2,1.4:R=32,48,64:L=64,128 prints the Pareto frontier (recall, dist-comps, p99) + one recommended (build params, ef) meeting the target at minimum measured distance-computations; --json emits Vec<TuneResult>.

11.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
Recommended point measured recall@10 >= 0.95 (within harness CI)
CostModel prediction error vs measured dist-comps <= 15% median over grid
Pareto monotonicity recall strictly increasing as dist-comps increases; no dominated points
min_ef_for_target correctness returned ef-1 fails target, ef passes (boundary verified)
Determinism fixed BuildParams.seed + grid → identical frontier across runs
No silent regression every reported point's measured recall recomputed, not predicted

11.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Expected
tune_sift10k.bin + gt_10.bin frontier ≥4 non-dominated points; recommended ef ∈ [40,120], recall ≥ 0.95
cost_calib.json predicted vs measured dist-comps table; median err ≤ 15%
pareto_golden.json byte-stable frontier under fixed seed
malformed: --target recall@0>=0.95 error: k must be ≥1
malformed: --target recall@10>=1.5 error: recall ∈ (0,1]
malformed: empty grid error: grid must yield ≥1 cell
degenerate: target unreachable at ef_hi report None + "target infeasible at ef≤{ef_hi}"

11.6 Pitfalls

  • Non-monotone recall in ef (ties / unreachable pockets) → bisection picks wrong ef. Root cause: tie-break nondeterminism or orphan nodes. Debug: plot recall(ef); fall back to linear scan; assert connectivity (Phase 9) before tuning.
  • Overfitting to self-query sample → inflated recall. Use held-out queries distinct from indexed vectors; flag if ground_truth derived from the indexed set.
  • Cost model ignores cache effects → kernel_ns grows with working-set size; calibrate kernel_ns per ef band, not once globally.
  • Grid rebuild explosion → |alpha|*|R|*|L| builds dominate runtime; cap cells, warn if >64, support --max-cells.
  • p99 noise on small samples → require SampleSet.len >= 1000 queries or widen CI; always report sample size.
  • Predicted-not-measured leak → recommending on predicted cost when measured violates target; recompute measured recall at the final point unconditionally.

11.7 Performance Concerns

  • Tuning cost dominated by grid rebuilds, not search; parallelize cells (rayon), share VectorStore read-only.
  • Bisection ≈ log2(ef_hi-ef_lo) search passes per cell; each pass O(|sample| * ef * R) dist-comps.
  • Cache RecallHarness.ground_truth once; reuse across all cells (target-independent, build-independent).
  • min_ef_for_target warm-up: discard first pass for latency, keep its dist-comps.

11.8 Edge Cases

  • Target met at ef_lo → return ef_lo, skip bisection.
  • Single-node / tiny graph → ef clamped to node count; recall=1.0 trivially.
  • All grid cells fail target → frontier returned, recommended=None, exit code 2.
  • Pareto ties (equal dist-comps, equal recall) → keep first by stable sort, dedup.
  • k > graph size → harness caps k; recall@k denominator adjusts.

11.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Target infeasible min_ef_for_target → None at ef_hi report best-achievable recall + ef_hi
Cost model drift >15% calibration residual check recalibrate per-band; warn, keep measured authoritative
Non-monotone ef recall(ef) decreasing step switch to linear scan, log warning
Build OOM in large grid cell builder error skip cell, mark TuneResult absent, continue

11.10 Operational Notes

  • annopt tune output stable across runs (seeded) → CI golden-diff on pareto_golden.json.
  • Recommended point feeds annopt search --ef N directly; emit copy-paste command line.
  • Log calibration residuals + sample size at info; frontier table at default verbosity.
  • Re-tune trigger: after Phase 10 consolidation, or when monitored-sample recall drifts below target.

24. Phase 12 — Persistence, mmap, and PQ compression (DiskANN-style rerank)

Crate: ann-store. Turns the in-memory CsrGraph + VectorStore into a versioned on-disk artifact, mmaps it for larger-than-RAM search, adds ProductQuantizer for 8–16x vector compression, and runs DiskANN-style beam search over PQ codes with a full-precision re-rank tail. NON-GOAL: no WAL, no transactions, no concurrent writers (single-node, write-once-then-mmap-read).

12.1 Objectives

  • Versioned, mmappable on-disk format for CsrGraph (offsets+edges) + VectorStore (f32 vectors) + optional PQ codes/codebooks; little-endian, fixed magic, alignment-aware.
  • mmap_graph(path) -> io::Result<MappedGraph>: zero-copy &[u32]/&[f32]/&[u8] views; search runs directly off the map, no full-vector RAM resident.
  • ProductQuantizer: train m_subspaces codebooks of ksub centroids (Lloyd k-means per subspace), encode f32 → u8 codes, asymmetric distance via per-query LUT.
  • DiskANN-style search: beam_search over PQ asymmetric distances → candidate superset, then full-precision (mmapped f32) re-rank of top-rerank_k → final top-k.
  • INVARIANT: save→mmap→search reproduces in-memory full-precision recall exactly (bit-identical results); PQ path trades bounded recall loss (≤ stated Δ) for measured memory reduction.

12.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per [P5] PQ §3 asymmetric distance computation, [P2] DiskANN §4 SSD sector beam-search + rerank, ADR-14 PQ compression, ADR-15 mmap access, INV-9 persistence round-trip determinism, P4-OptimizedGraphImmutableSendSync)

Files (under ann-store/src/): format.rs, mmap.rs, pq.rs, rerank.rs, delete.rs, lib.rs.

On-disk layout (format.rs), little-endian, 64-byte aligned sections:

Header : magic "ANNOPT01"(8) | version u32 | metric u8 | graph_kind u8 | flags u16
         n u32 | dim u32 | entry_point u32 | max_degree u32 | crc32 u32 | reserved
[CSR]  : offsets[n+1] u32     (64B aligned)
         edges[offsets[n]] u32
[VEC]  : vectors[n*dim] f32   (64B aligned, SIMD-loadable)
[PQ?]  : flags&PQ  => pq_m u32 | pq_ksub u32 | codebooks[m*ksub*(dim/m)] f32 | codes[n*m] u8
[DEL?] : flags&DEL => DeleteSet bits[ceil(n/8)] u8
  • save_graph(g:&CsrGraph, store:&VectorStore, path:&Path) -> io::Result<()>: write header (crc over body), pad each section to 64B, fsync. Variant save_graph_pq(g, store, pq, codes, path) sets flags&PQ.
  • mmap.rs: MappedGraph { map: Mmap, header: Header } with accessors offsets()->&[u32], edges()->&[u32], vectors()->&[f32], codes()->Option<&[u8]>, as_csr()->CsrGraphView. Slices via bytemuck::cast_slice after alignment assert.
  • pq.rs: ProductQuantizer{ m_subspaces, ksub, codebooks: Vec<f32> }. train(store,&PqParams) k-means per subspace (iters, seed); encode(v:&[f32])->SmallVec<[u8;M]>; distance_table(q:&[f32])->Vec<f32> (m×ksub LUT); asym_distance(code,&lut)->Distance = Σ lut[s*ksub+code[s]].
  • rerank.rs: pq_beam_search(mapped,&SearchParams{k,ef,..}, rerank_k, ctx): beam search using asym_distance to gather rerank_k≥k candidates, recompute exact Distance on mmapped f32, sort, return top-k.

12.3 Deliverable

annopt build … --save out.annopt (full precision) and --pq m=16,ksub=256 --save out.annopt.pq; annopt search --mmap out.annopt[.pq] [--rerank-k N]. Library: save_graph, mmap_graph, ProductQuantizer, pq_beam_search. Round-trip + PQ recall report.

12.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
save→mmap→search vs in-memory (full precision) recall@10 identical, results bit-identical
PQ memory reduction (dim=128, m=16 → 16B vs 512B/vec) ≥ 8x (≥16x at m=8) on vector bytes, reported
PQ+rerank recall@10 (SIFT-analog), rerank_k=10·k ≥ 0.95 of full-precision recall
mmap cold-start search (no preload) works with RSS < full vector size
crc32 mismatch / bad magic / truncation typed load error, no panic, no OOB read

12.5 Conformance Fixtures

Fixture Expected
roundtrip_1k.annopt reload → as_csr() equals source CsrGraph; recall == in-mem
pq_sift10k.annopt.pq PQ recall@10 ≥ 0.95·full; mem reduction ≥8x in report
pq_codebook.golden trained codebooks byte-stable for fixed seed
bad_magic.bin Err(LoadError::BadMagic)
truncated.annopt offsets[n+1] > file len → Err(Truncated), no OOB
crc_corrupt.annopt flipped body byte → Err(CrcMismatch)
misaligned.annopt unaligned VEC section → Err(Misaligned) (not UB)

12.6 Pitfalls

  • Unaligned mmap cast → cast_slice panics / UB on f32 view. Root: section not 64B padded. Fix: assert ptr % 64 == 0 at load, pad on write. Debug: log section offsets.
  • Endianness drift → silent garbage on BE host. Root: raw memcpy. Fix: magic+version gate, LE-only documented; reject mismatched version.
  • PQ LUT staleness → wrong distances. Root: reusing distance_table across queries. Fix: rebuild LUT per query; treat as per-query state.
  • rerank_k too small → PQ recall collapses. Root: true NN ranked beyond rerank_k by approx distance. Fix: rerank_k ≥ 5–10·k; report recall-vs-rerank_k curve.
  • k-means empty cluster → NaN centroid → NaN distance. Root: degenerate subspace. Fix: reseed empty centroid to farthest point; assert no NaN in codebooks.
  • mmap page faults dominate latency → cold p99 spikes. Root: random edge/vector access. Fix: madvise(WILLNEED) on entry neighborhood; report cold vs warm.

12.7 Performance Concerns

  • PQ asym distance = gather+sum over m LUT lookups (~m loads) vs dim FMAs; wins when dim/m ≫ 1; keep LUT in L1 (m·ksub·4B = 16KB at m=16,ksub=256).
  • mmap removes vector RAM but adds page-fault latency; sequential CSR edges layout improves locality; madvise(RANDOM) for vectors, SEQUENTIAL for offsets.
  • Re-rank cost = rerank_k full-precision distances → dominates large-rerank_k queries; tune for recall/latency Pareto (ties to Phase 11 Autotuner).

12.8 Edge Cases

  • dim % m_subspaces != 0 → reject in PqParams validation (sub-dim must divide dim).
  • n=0 / empty graph → header valid, zero sections, search returns empty.
  • PQ flag set but codes absent / count ≠ n·m → Err(PqShapeMismatch).
  • DeleteSet present → tombstoned ids skipped in beam expansion and re-rank.
  • Filesystem without mmap support → fallback read-based loader behind same MappedGraph API.

12.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Recovery
Torn write (crash mid-save) crc32 over body reject load; rebuild from in-mem graph
Codebook/codes drift (mismatched PQ versions) pq_m/ksub in header vs LUT Err(PqVersionMismatch)
mmap SIGBUS (file truncated after map) length check at map + bounds in accessors Err(Truncated), never deref past len
Re-rank selects tombstoned vector filter via DeleteSet before sort excluded from results

12.10 Operational Notes

  • Format version bumped on any layout change; loader refuses unknown versions (no silent skew).
  • annopt diagnose --mmap reports section sizes, PQ params, achieved mem-reduction, crc status.
  • Recommend full-precision save for ≤ RAM datasets; PQ+mmap for > RAM; document 8–16x reduction and measured recall delta in the build manifest emitted alongside the artifact.
  • Artifacts immutable post-save; incremental maintenance (Phase 10) operates on the in-memory form then re-saves.

25. Phase 13 — Conformance, differential testing, fuzzing, benchmarks

The terminal "done" gate: stand up the full validation surface, prove the section-4 recall/latency targets on real-corpus analogs, prove behavioral parity vs hnswlib + DiskANN, and prove no panics under adversarial fuzz. Until this phase is green there is no shippable release.

13.1 Objectives

  • Load SIFT1M / GIST1M / DEEP-subset analogs (fvecs/bvecs + precomputed GT ivecs) into conformance/; run ann-benchmarks-style recall@k-vs-latency protocol; assert section-4 targets with regression gates (recall floor + latency/dist-comp ceiling).
  • Differential harness: compare recall@10 and neighbor-set Jaccard vs hnswlib + DiskANN on a shared corpus at matched build params.
  • Three fuzz targets — build (never panic on adversarial vectors/params), search (never panic, always len ≤ k), optimize (never regress recall, never violate degree/connectivity invariants).
  • Criterion benchmarks: build throughput, search QPS, per-optimizer-pass cost; emit machine-readable Pareto report.

13.2 Detailed Implementation Steps

(per [P7] ann-benchmarks §3 recall@k protocol + fairness rules, ADR-19 differential oracle, ADR-18 fuzzing, ADR-20 benchmarking, P1-RecallMeasuredNeverAssumed, INV-6 optimizer guarantee — final gate)

File / module Adds
ann-store/src/fvecs.rs read_fvecs(&Path)->io::Result<VectorStore>, read_bvecs (u8→f32), read_ivecs->Vec<Vec<VectorId>> (GT)
conformance/datasets.toml per-dataset: dim, n, Metric, k, base/query/gt paths, recall floor, p99 ceiling
ann-search (RecallHarness) load_ground_truth(&[Vec<VectorId>]), recall_at_k(&[SearchResult],gt,k)->f32, jaccard(a,b)->f32
xtask/src/conformance.rs sweep ef ∈ {16,32,64,128,256}; record (recall@k, mean DistanceCounter, p50/p99 µs); assert gates
xtask/src/differential.rs drive vendored refs/{hnswlib,diskann} binaries; parse neighbor IDs; diff
fuzz/fuzz_targets/{build,search,optimize}.rs cargo-fuzz/libFuzzer harnesses
ann-build/ann-search/ann-optimize/benches/*.rs criterion groups
  • Protocol: build once per (dataset, BuildParams); per ef run all queries single-thread, core-pinned; wall µs = min-of-N per query (kill noise); record DistanceCounter per query via greedy_search/beam_search.
  • Differential: identical corpus; map GraphKind::Hnsw{m,ef_construction} ↔ hnswlib (M,efConstruction), GraphKind::Vamana{r,l,alpha} ↔ DiskANN; compare top-k ID sets (order-insensitive Jaccard) + aggregate recall.
  • Fuzz optimize: arbitrary valid Graph → snapshot GraphDiagnostics → run occlusion_prune/degree_rebalance/connect_components → assert OPTIMIZER GUARANTEE.

13.3 Deliverable

cargo test -p conformance --release green on all dataset buckets; xtask differential reports parity within tolerance; cargo fuzz run {build,search,optimize} zero crashes for the CI budget; cargo bench emits target/criterion + conformance/report.json (recall/latency Pareto per dataset). annopt bench --dataset sift1m reproduces the published numbers.

13.4 Acceptance Criteria

Metric Target
SIFT1M HNSW recall@10 @ ef=64 ≥ 0.95
SIFT1M HNSW p99 query latency (1 thread, M=16) ≤ 800 µs
GIST1M (d=960) recall@10 @ ef=128 ≥ 0.90
DEEP-subset Vamana recall@10 (R=64, α=1.2) ≥ 0.94
Differential recall delta vs hnswlib/DiskANN within ±0.01 absolute
Mean neighbor-set Jaccard vs refs ≥ 0.90
Fuzz crashes (build/search/optimize), CI budget ≥ 5 min each 0
Optimizer recall regression on diagnostic sample 0 (monotone non-decreasing)

13.5 Conformance Fixtures

File Expected
conformance/sift1m/{base.fvecs(1M×128),query.fvecs(10k),gt.ivecs(10k×100)} parse → VectorStore len=1_000_000 dim=128
conformance/gist1m/*.fvecs (1M×960) recall sweep matches report.json golden ±0.005
conformance/deep10k/* (subset analog) fast CI bucket, recall@10 ≥ 0.94
golden/sift1m_ef64.json {recall, mean_dist_comps, p99_us} regression baseline
Malformed: truncated fvecs (dim header > remaining bytes) Err(InvalidFormat), no panic
Malformed: GT k=100 but only 50 ids/row Err(GroundTruthShape)
Malformed: query dim ≠ base dim Err(DimMismatch) at harness load

13.6 Pitfalls

  • GT metric mismatch: SIFT GT is L2, DEEP analogs often IP/Cosine; wrong Metric silently tanks recall → assert metric in datasets.toml matches dataset, log it.
  • bvecs sign: SIFT bvecs are unsigned 0–255; reading as i8 wraps → recall collapses. Debug: histogram raw bytes, expect [0,255].
  • Timer warm-up: first queries pay page-fault/cache-cold cost, inflating p99 → discard warm-up batch, min-of-N per query, pin core (core_affinity).
  • Differential param skew: hnswlib ef includes the result set; off-by-k makes recall diff look like a bug → align definitions, validate on a 1k toy corpus first.
  • Fuzz false-negative: a corpus seeded only with valid graphs misses the NaN/Inf vector path → seed optimize/build fuzzers with NaN/Inf/zero vectors via arbitrary.
  • Jaccard ties: equidistant neighbors → ref and ours legitimately pick different IDs; compare Distance values, not just IDs, treating distance-tie sets as equal.

13.7 Performance Concerns

  • Conformance SIFT1M build < 10 min release single-machine; cache the built CsrGraph in target/conformance-cache/ keyed by BuildParams hash to skip rebuild per CI run.
  • mmap_graph GIST1M (960-d f32, ~3.6 GB) — measure cold vs warm, report both; exercise PQ path for the memory ceiling.
  • Criterion: sample_size=50, 3 s warm-up; isolate SIMD DistanceKernel bench from graph-walk bench so kernel regressions are attributable.
  • Differential ref binaries dominate wall-time → run parity on 10k subset; full 1M only for our own published numbers.

13.8 Edge Cases

  • Empty query file → harness returns recall NaN guard → report n/a, not 0.0.
  • k > dataset size (tiny buckets) → clamp k, recall over available.
  • All-identical vectors → exact KNN ambiguous; recall defined via Distance equivalence classes.
  • ef < k → greedy_search/beam_search still return len ≤ k (Phase 5 invariant); conformance asserts it.
  • Query == base vector (self-match at distance 0) → configurable include_self for GT membership.

13.9 Failure Analysis

Failure Detection Action
Recall below floor gate assert in conformance.rs fail CI, dump per-query recall histogram + worst-100 queries
Latency regression > 10% vs golden compare golden/*.json fail, attach criterion diff
Differential Jaccard < 0.90 diff harness dump divergent query IDs + both neighbor sets w/ distances
Fuzz crash libFuzzer artifact minimize, commit as seed under fuzz/corpus/regressions/
Optimizer recall regression snapshot before/after in optimize fuzzer + conformance reject pass, flag non-compliant with OPTIMIZER GUARANTEE

13.10 Operational Notes

  • Datasets large + license-restricted → not committed; xtask fetch-datasets downloads + verifies SHA256 into conformance/<name>/; CI uses cached artifact, gracefully skips (not fails) with a loud warning if absent.
  • Fuzz budget: nightly runs each target ≥ 5 min; PR runs ≥ 60 s + full regression-corpus replay (must always pass).
  • report.json published per release tag; Pareto frontier plotted by scripts/plot_pareto.py (Python, plotting only — non-load-bearing).
  • Golden baselines updated only via explicit xtask bless with reviewer sign-off; never auto-updated, else the regression gates are meaningless.
  • Done gate = all acceptance rows green + 3 fuzz targets clean for CI budget + differential within tolerance + report.json published; only then is project 315 complete.

Threat Model

Scope: adversarial + accidental failure across the spine's three boundaries. Subject = the ANN index/optimizer, not auth/transport (out of scope; assume caller-side ACLs). Adversary classes: (A) untrusted vector/query supplier feeding the ann-index API; (B) concurrent caller mix (many readers || one writer); (C) operator/tooling driving annox; (D) attacker with read/write access to the on-disk IndexFile or its directory. Asset = index integrity (recall correctness, no corruption, no panics, bounded resource use) + availability of the search hot path.

26.1 Trust-boundary analysis

Boundary             Inside (trusted)              Outside (untrusted)            Enforcement point
-------------------- ---------------------------- ------------------------------ ----------------------------
API / input          built graph, codebooks,      caller-supplied dim, f32       ann-index build/search/
                     BuildParams/SearchParams      payloads (NaN/Inf), ids        insert/delete (validate)
Concurrency          immutable/epoch graph,        interleaving of N readers      ann-index locking + epoch
                     per-node lock discipline      with 1 writer                  reclamation (Phase 12)
Persistence          in-memory graph (runtime      bytes on disk: IndexFile,      ann-index open()/recovery
                     source of truth)              DiskANN sectors, snapshots     (header magic+ver+CRC)

Rules: every value crossing a boundary is validated/sanitized exactly once at the named point; downstream crates (ann-graph/search/optimize/quantize) assume validated inputs and may use checked-only-in-debug indexing on the hot path. The in-memory graph — not the mmap file — is authoritative; open() reconstructs and re-validates.

26.2 Threat catalog

Each: vector → mitigation → detection → telemetry → recovery. T-IDs referenced by Failure Modes / Operational Readiness.

T1 — Algorithmic-complexity DoS (parameter blow-up)

Vector: caller sets ef_search, M, max_degree, ef_construction, dim, or order huge → O(ef * degree) search / O(N*efC) build explodes; one query starves all readers. Mitigation: reject at API against spine hard caps — dim<=4096, max_degree<=512, ef_search<=4096, order<=2^31; clamp beam_width<=ef_search; per-search wall-clock budget; visited-set cap = order. Detection: bounds check before allocation; counter of clamped/rejected calls. Telemetry: rejected_param_total{param}, search_latency p99, frontier_pop_count histogram. Recovery: return Err(ParamOutOfRange{param,max}); no partial state. Fixture: ef_search=u32::MAX, dim=1<<20.

T2 — Adversarial graph topology (slow-search inputs)

Vector: crafted/clustered vectors → degenerate graph (long paths, hub nodes) inflating frontier pops far beyond recall target; or insert order chosen to maximize degree churn. Mitigation: DegreeBound caps out-degree at M/M0; RobustPrune(alpha) + RNG occlusion keep diameter low; ConnectivityRepair bounds path length; per-search visited cap aborts pathological traversals. Detection: pop_count > k_factor * ef_search anomaly; degree-distribution skew at consolidate. Telemetry: avg/p99 hops-to-converge, max degree observed, hub_node_count. Recovery: LayoutReorder + EdgePrune pass; abort offending query with budget Err, index unharmed.

T3 — Malformed / out-of-spec input (NaN, Inf, dim mismatch, subnormals)

Vector: NaN/Inf vector poisons distances (NaN compares false → breaks heap ordering, ranks everywhere/nowhere); wrong dim reads OOB; -0.0/subnormals skew metrics; zero-norm vector under Cosine → divide-by-zero. Mitigation: validate(v): len==dim, all finite (reject NaN/Inf), Cosine norm>epsilon (else Err ZeroNorm); normalize on ingest for Cosine. Detection: is_finite scan (SIMD) per inserted/queried vector at the API. Telemetry: rejected_nonfinite_total, zero_norm_total, dim_mismatch_total. Recovery: Err(InvalidVector{reason}); never enters graph/storage. Fixture: f32::NAN, f32::INFINITY, all-zero under Cosine, dim=dim-1/dim+1.

T4 — Memory exhaustion (OOM via allocation)

Vector: build of huge N, oversized PQ codebooks (m_subksub_dim), or many concurrent SearchStates each holding visited+heaps → unbounded heap. Mitigation: pre-flight memory estimate (N*(M24 + dim*4) ≈ spine model) checked vs a configured budget before build; SearchState pooled + reused (zero steady-state alloc per spine); PQ params bounded (k<=256, m_sub<=dim). Detection: estimator vs budget gate; allocator high-water counter. Telemetry: rss_bytes, searchstate_pool_inflight, build_peak_bytes. Recovery: Err(BudgetExceeded{need,have}) before allocating; no torn build. Fixture: order near 2^31, dim 4096, concurrency=1024.

T5 — Disk / inode exhaustion (DiskANN sectors, snapshots)

Vector: repeated save()/snapshot or large IndexFile + DiskANN sector layout fills volume mid-write → truncated file. Mitigation: write to temp + fsync + atomic rename (no in-place mutation of live file); snapshot retention cap; estimate file size before write; bounded sector count = order. Detection: pre-write free-space check; short-write/ENOSPC propagated as Err. Telemetry: disk_free_bytes, snapshot_count, last_save_bytes. Recovery: failed save leaves prior IndexFile intact (rename is atomic); in-memory graph still authoritative. Fixture: ENOSPC injection on temp write.

T6 — Panic on adversarial input (availability)

Vector: any panic (index OOB, unwrap on None, integer overflow on offsets/ids, slice misalign) aborts the process → one bad request kills all readers. Mitigation: API surface is Result-only (no unwrap/expect/panic! in ann-index public paths); checked arithmetic for offsets/CSR indices/VectorId↔NodeId; debug-mode bounds asserts, release-mode pre-validated invariants. catch_unwind firewall at the request boundary as defense-in-depth. Detection: continuous fuzzing (cargo-fuzz) of build/search/insert/delete + parser of IndexFile bytes; #[deny] on arithmetic overflow in CI. Telemetry: panic_caught_total (must stay 0), fuzz corpus crash count. Recovery: catch_unwind → Err(Internal), request fails, server lives. Fixture: fuzz targets per public fn; corpus seeded with T3/T7 cases.

T7 — Persistence / state corruption (untrusted IndexFile bytes)

Vector: attacker or bit-rot supplies IndexFile with bogus offsets, out-of-range NodeIds, degree>cap, cyclic/garbage CSR, wrong dim → open() builds a graph that reads OOB or loops forever. Mitigation: file header = magic + format-version + dim + order + metric + per-section CRC32C; open() validates header, bounds-checks every CSR offset/neighbor id (< order), enforces degree<=max_degree, rejects version mismatch; treat mmap region as untrusted (no transmute of unvalidated structs). Detection: CRC mismatch, offset/id range check, monotonic-offset check on CSR. Telemetry: open_crc_fail_total, open_reject_total{reason}, format_version. Recovery: refuse to open (Err(CorruptIndex{section})); fall back to last good snapshot; rebuild from source vectors as last resort. Fixture: flip CRC byte, offset past neighbors.len(), NodeId==order.

T8 — Torn writes / partial persistence (crash mid-mutation)

Vector: crash during save()/consolidate()/sector update leaves IndexFile half-written or tombstones applied without neighbor-list rewrite → inconsistent on next open(). Mitigation: atomic publish (temp+fsync+rename); never mutate live file in place; consolidate() stages new neighbor lists then swaps; tombstones are append-only until consolidation; recovery log / generation counter in header. Detection: open() generation/CRC + consistency pass (entry_point valid, no neighbor → deleted node). Telemetry: recovery_invoked_total, dangling_neighbor_total at open, generation. Recovery: roll forward to last complete generation; re-run consolidate idempotently. Fixture: kill -9 mid-consolidate, mid-save.

T9 — Concurrency hazards (reader/writer races)

Vector: writer mutates adjacency while readers traverse → data race / use-after-free of freed Neighbors; lost update on bidirectional link + prune; ABA on node slot reuse after delete. Mitigation (spine concurrency model): searches read-only over an immutable/epoch-protected graph; single writer with fine-grained per-node locks; epoch-based reclamation defers freeing of replaced adjacency until no reader holds the epoch; lock ordering by NodeId to prevent deadlock; CSR frozen form immutable. Detection: TSan/loom/Miri in CI on mutation primitives; assert no reader observes a half-linked node. Telemetry: epoch_lag, lock_wait_p99, reclamation_backlog. Recovery: writer backpressure if reclamation backlog grows; readers never block writer correctness. Fixture: loom model of insert||search, delete-then-insert ABA, concurrent prune of shared neighbor.

T10 — Connectivity / unreachability attack (silent recall loss)

Vector: adversarial delete/insert/prune sequence orphans live nodes from the entry point → searches silently miss them (recall drops, no error); approximation (PQ/Bloom in repair) wrongly declares a node reachable. Mitigation: connectivity invariant — every live node reachable from entry_point; ConnectivityRepair runs reachability BFS and reconnects orphans after deletes/prunes; approximations may add false positives in candidacy but must NEVER make a live node permanently unreachable (exact reachability check governs repair). Detection: periodic/post-consolidate reachability BFS; recall canary vs brute-force oracle on a held-out query set. Telemetry: orphan_count, unreachable_live_total (alert if >0), canary_recall@10. Recovery: ConnectivityRepair pass; if canary recall < target, EdgePrune/Diversify rerun or rebuild. Fixture: delete entry-point neighborhood; prune to disconnect a cluster.

T11 — Quantization / plan poisoning (recall + cost integrity)

Vector: stale or adversarially trained PQ/SQ codebooks (drifted distribution) make ADC distances wrong → search returns far vectors as near (poisoned ranking); ParamTune fed manipulated gt/queries picks SearchParams that look good but underperform in prod. Mitigation: PqCode/codebook versioned + bound to index generation (reject mismatched codebook); always full-precision rerank of top candidates (ADC only narrows, never finalizes); tune_for_recall uses brute_force_knn-derived ground truth, not caller-supplied rankings; Pareto points recorded with the gt hash they were measured against. Detection: codebook_version mismatch; rerank disagreement rate (ADC top-k vs reranked top-k) anomaly; gt hash check. Telemetry: rerank_overturn_rate, codebook_version, tune_gt_hash. Recovery: re-train PQ (pq_train) on current data; re-tune; reject mismatched codebook at open(). Fixture: codebook from a different distribution; gt with injected wrong neighbors.

T12 — Supply-chain risk (dependencies)

Vector: malicious/vulnerable transitive crate in the SIMD/mmap/CLI deps; yanked or typosquatted dependency. Mitigation: minimal dependency surface (core no_std+alloc); cargo-deny (deny.toml) bans by advisory (RUSTSEC), enforces license allow-list, denies duplicate/unknown sources, pins git deps; Cargo.lock committed; SIMD via std::arch intrinsics (no FFI). Detection: cargo deny check in CI (advisories+bans+licenses+sources); cargo-audit on schedule. Telemetry: CI deny/audit pass/fail, dependency count, MSRV (1.78) drift check. Recovery: pin/replace/remove offending crate; vendored fallback for critical path. Fixture: deny.toml with a known-bad advisory id (CI must fail).

T13 — Operator / configuration mistakes

Vector: wrong Metric vs how vectors were embedded (L2 vs Cosine) → garbage recall; mismatched build vs query dim; overwriting a good index via annox build; running optimize passes with bad alpha (<1) degrading graph; deleting wrong snapshot. Mitigation: Metric + dim + format-version persisted in IndexFile header and checked at every search/insert; annox refuses build over an existing file without --force; alpha validated alpha>=1 (RobustPrune); destructive CLI ops require confirmation; PassReport surfaces recall_before/after so a harmful pass is visible. Detection: header mismatch Err; PassReport recall_after < recall_before flag; pre-op dry-run/inspect. Telemetry: config_mismatch_total, pass recall delta, destructive_op_total. Recovery: refuse mismatched search; revert pass (graph snapshot before optimize) / restore snapshot. Fixture: build L2 then query Cosine; alpha=0.5; build over existing file.

26.3 Domain-specific risks summary

Risk                         Primary control                              Telemetry signal
---------------------------- -------------------------------------------- -----------------------
Data/history leakage         no raw vectors in error strings/logs (ids    log_payload_bytes==0
                             only); search returns ids+dist, not vectors  on error paths
Recall regression (silent)   brute-force canary + PassReport gates        canary_recall@10
Plan/cost poisoning          oracle-derived gt; rerank finalizes          rerank_overturn_rate
Torn writes                  temp+fsync+atomic-rename; generation ctr     recovery_invoked_total
Unreachable live nodes       reachability BFS invariant (T10)             unreachable_live_total

26.4 Residual risks & assumptions

  • Trusts the OS for mmap page integrity and fsync durability; bit-rot beyond CRC granularity (within a CRC'd section) is undetectable until re-validation.
  • Single-node model (spine non-goal: no sharding) → no defense against a host-level compromise; assumes caller authenticates/authorizes (auth out of scope).
  • Timing side-channels on distance/recall not addressed (not a confidentiality target).
  • Approximate recall is inherent: ANN may miss true neighbors even with no adversary; only the connectivity invariant (T10) bounds worst-case silent loss, not statistical recall — covered by canary gates, not a hard guarantee.

Failure Modes and Recovery

Taxonomy of failure classes for the OPTIMIZER + index runtime. Each: symptoms / root cause / detection / auto-recovery / manual recovery / operator action / forensic artifacts / long-term mitigation. Gravest class: F2 — Silent Recall Regression (correct API behavior, results merely wrong/incomplete; no error raised). Severity scale: SEV1 silent-wrong-or-data-loss, SEV2 unavailability, SEV3 degraded.

Failure class table

ID Class Sev Trigger surface
F1 Input / parse error SEV3 ann-index API: dim mismatch, NaN/Inf, oversized k/ef
F2 Silent recall regression (wrong/missing results) SEV1 optimize passes, quantization, consolidate, tombstone leak
F3 Resource exhaustion (OOM / degree blowup / fd) SEV2 build, ParamTune, mmap, concurrent insert
F4 Crash mid-operation (build/consolidate/save) SEV2 power loss, panic, OOM-kill during mutation+fsync
F5 Concurrency anomaly (race / torn read / lost edge) SEV1 concurrent search ∥ insert/delete/consolidate
F6 Persistence corruption (IndexFile / sector layout) SEV1 bad disk, partial write, bitrot, version skew
F7 Performance cliff (latency/QPS collapse) SEV3 ef_search drift, cold mmap, fragmentation, NUMA
F8 Connectivity loss (orphan unreachable nodes) SEV1 over-prune, delete entry_point, Bloom false-neg

F1 — Input / parse error

  • Symptoms: caller gets typed error; no mutation.
  • Root cause: dim != index.dim; non-finite floats; k>order; ef_search>4096; dim>4096; degree request>512.
  • Detection: validate at ann-index boundary BEFORE touching graph — validate_query(q) -> Result<(), InputError> checks len==dim, all finite (is_finite), bounds vs hard caps.
  • Auto-recovery: reject, return Err(InputError::{DimMismatch,NonFinite,OutOfRange}); index untouched (no partial insert).
  • Manual: caller sanitizes (drop NaN rows, clip dim).
  • Operator: monitor InputError rate; spike ⇒ upstream embedder broke.
  • Forensic: structured log {op, dim_seen, dim_expected, first_bad_idx}.
  • Mitigation: schema pin (dim+metric in IndexFile header), reject at open if mismatch.

F2 — Silent recall regression (HIGHEST SEVERITY)

  • Symptoms: search returns k results, no error, but recall@10 drops below 0.95 target; users see "missing" neighbors. Most dangerous: it looks healthy.
  • Root causes (ranked): (1) over-aggressive EdgePrune/DegreeBound severs needed edges; (2) RobustPrune alpha too low (alpha<1 illegal; ~1.0 under-diversifies, long-range edges lost); (3) HnswHeuristic keepPrunedConnections=false leaving under-degree nodes; (4) PQ codebook under-trained (too few k-means iters) ⇒ ADC distances mis-rank, rerank pool too small; (5) tombstone leak (deleted ids still in results / live ids filtered); (6) ParamTune fits ef_search to a stale/wrong ground-truth set; (7) entry_point/medoid stale after consolidate.
  • Detection (MUST be continuous, not just at build):
    • Every OptimizerPass emits PassReport{recall_before, recall_after}; gate: abort/rollback pass if recall_after < recall_before − epsilon (epsilon=0.005).
    • Shadow eval: sample N=1000 held-out queries, compare vs brute_force_knn oracle on a snapshot; alert if recall@10 < 0.95.
    • Quantized vs full-precision divergence: assert PQ-rerank recall@10 >= 0.90.
  • Auto-recovery: pass framework is transactional — snapshot adjacency delta per pass; recall gate failure ⇒ revert AdjacencyStore to pre-pass CSR; ParamTune rejects any ParetoPoint below target.
  • Manual: re-run failing pass with relaxed params (raise alpha to 1.2, raise max_degree, enable keepPrunedConnections); rebuild PQ with more iters / larger rerank K; refresh ground truth then re-tune.
  • Operator: page on shadow-recall breach; freeze further optimize passes; pin last-good Snapshot.
  • Forensic: per-pass PassReport history (edges_removed/added, recall delta, p99 delta); recall-vs-QPS Pareto trace; PQ training loss curve; ground-truth checksum.
  • Mitigation: recall is a hard CI gate (Phase 13 regression); never ship a pass without before/after recall; oracle GT versioned + checksummed; alpha lower-bounded at 1.0.

F2 debugging methodology (gravest-class playbook)

  1. Confirm it's real, not GT staleness: recompute brute_force_knn oracle on the current dataset snapshot; if recall recovers vs fresh GT, root cause = stale GT in tuner — fix and stop.
  2. Bisect the pipeline: measure recall after each stage — raw graph → each OptimizerPass (in order) → quantized → reranked. The first stage with a drop is the culprit (PassReport deltas pinpoint it).
  3. If a pass: dump edges_removed for sample nodes; check RNG/occlusion rule kept >=1 short edge per direction; check no node fell below min-degree; re-run with extendCandidates / higher alpha.
  4. If quantization: compare ADC distance vs exact for top-100; large rank inversions ⇒ retrain PQ (more iters, check sub_dim split) or widen rerank pool.
  5. If consolidate/tombstone: verify search filter drops only Tombstone::Deleted; scan adjacency for dangling NodeIds → live tombstoned targets.
  6. Connectivity cross-check (overlaps F8): run reachability BFS from entry_point; unreachable live nodes ⇒ those queries silently miss them.
  7. Lock-in: add a fixture reproducing the regression to the differential-vs-brute-force suite before unfreezing optimize.

F3 — Resource exhaustion

  • Symptoms: OOM-kill mid-build; alloc spikes; "too many open files"; degree vector grows unbounded.
  • Root cause: efC*order candidate buffers; ParamTune sweeping large M×efC grid in memory; per-node lock table; unbounded neighbor backfill ignoring max_degree; mmap of giant IndexFile + page cache.
  • Detection: pre-build memory estimate ≈ N*(M24 + dim*4) (≈0.6 GB SIFT1M) checked vs budget; degree assert <=512; RSS watchdog.
  • Auto-recovery: hard caps enforced (max_degree 512, ef 4096, dim 4096, order 2^31) — reject before alloc; ParamTune streams points, frees per candidate.
  • Manual: lower M/efC; build in shards; raise ulimit -n.
  • Operator: set cgroup memory limit; alert on RSS > 0.8 budget.
  • Forensic: peak-RSS log, per-phase alloc counters, degree histogram.
  • Mitigation: zero-alloc steady-state search (reused SearchState); arena for build; bounded SmallVec adjacency.

F4 — Crash mid-operation (recovery path)

  • Symptoms: process dies during build/consolidate/save; on restart IndexFile partial.
  • Root cause: power loss / panic / OOM-kill between mutation and fsync; non-atomic save.
  • Detection on open(): header magic+version+CRC; trailing commit-record presence; mmap length vs header-declared length.
  • Auto-recovery: atomic save = write to temp file → fsync → rename (POSIX atomic); commit record (sequence no + CRC) written last; on open, if commit record absent/bad ⇒ fall back to last valid Snapshot. In-memory graph is runtime source of truth, so a crash before save loses only the unsaved delta (replay from op-log if present).
  • Manual: annox inspect --recover selects newest consistent Snapshot.
  • Operator: keep last K snapshots; verify free space before save.
  • Forensic: commit-record seq, partial-file size, last-good snapshot id.
  • Mitigation: WAL/op-log for inserts since last snapshot; periodic snapshot cadence; fsync ordering (data before rename).

F5 — Concurrency anomaly

  • Symptoms: search returns torn neighbor list, missing recently-inserted node, or duplicate; rare panic; use-after-free risk.
  • Root cause: reader sees half-updated Neighbors mid-insert; writer prunes edge a reader is traversing; consolidate frees a node under a live searcher; entry_point swapped non-atomically.
  • Detection: loom/ThreadSanitizer model checks (Phase 12); invariant assert: visited set never contains freed NodeId.
  • Auto-recovery: concurrency model — many readers over immutable/epoch-protected graph ∥ one writer; per-node locks for insert; epoch-based reclamation so a node is freed only after all readers in its epoch retire; entry_point updated via atomic pointer swap.
  • Manual: none at runtime (correctness by construction); if anomaly observed ⇒ bug, revert to RwLock-coarse mode flag.
  • Operator: enable epoch-debug counters; watch retire backlog.
  • Forensic: epoch counters, lock-contention histogram, loom trace seed.
  • Mitigation: readers never mutate; consolidate publishes new CSR via atomic swap then reclaims old after grace; immutable frozen CSR for read path.

F6 — Persistence corruption

  • Symptoms: open() panics or yields garbage neighbors; CRC mismatch; DiskANN sector reads wrong vectors.
  • Root cause: bitrot, partial write (no atomic rename), version skew, sector misalignment.
  • Detection: per-region CRC32C (header, offsets, neighbors, vectors, codebooks); version+metric in header; sector-boundary assert.
  • Auto-recovery: refuse to mmap a CRC-failing region ⇒ load last Snapshot; reject on version/metric mismatch (no silent reinterpret).
  • Manual: annox inspect reports corrupt region; rebuild from source vectors if no good snapshot.
  • Operator: store on checksummed FS / replicate snapshots offhost.
  • Forensic: CRC report per region, header dump, byte offset of first mismatch.
  • Mitigation: CRC every region, atomic rename save, format version gate, periodic scrub.

F7 — Performance cliff

  • Symptoms: p99 jumps from <3 ms to tens of ms; QPS collapses though recall steady.
  • Root cause: ef_search drifted upward; cold mmap (page faults) post-restart; graph fragmentation after many inserts/deletes hurting cache/SSD locality; NUMA-remote pages; PQ rerank pool too large.
  • Detection: latency histogram (p50/p95/p99) per build; QPS regression gate vs baseline; page-fault counter.
  • Auto-recovery: adaptive ef tied to recall target (ParamTune), not hand-set; warm-up scan after open to fault pages.
  • Manual: run LayoutReorder (BFS/Gorder) to restore locality; run Compact after bulk deletes; lower ef to last Pareto point.
  • Operator: pin threads/memory to one NUMA node; pre-warm on deploy.
  • Forensic: latency histogram, page-fault delta, fragmentation ratio (CSR gap %), ef_search in effect.
  • Mitigation: periodic LayoutReorder, fragmentation alarm, Pareto-pinned ef, build/memory/latency regression gates (Phase 13).

F8 — Connectivity loss (orphans)

  • Symptoms: subset of live nodes never returned for any query (recall caps below 1.0 regardless of ef).
  • Root cause: over-prune severs last in-edge; deleting entry_point/medoid without re-election; Bloom/PQ approximation marks a needed node "skip".
  • Detection: ConnectivityRepair reachability BFS from entry_point — count unreachable live nodes (invariant: 0).
  • Auto-recovery: ConnectivityRepair pass re-links orphans (connect to nearest reachable via search); entry_point re-elected to a high-degree central node on deletion.
  • Manual: force-run ConnectivityRepair; if entry_point deleted, set new medoid.
  • Operator: alert if unreachable count > 0 after any optimize/consolidate.
  • Forensic: orphan node list, in/out-degree at orphan, entry_point id history.
  • Mitigation (invariant): every live node reachable from entry_point; approximations may slow but must never make a node permanently unreachable; ConnectivityRepair mandatory after EdgePrune and consolidate.

Operational Readiness

Framing: annopt ships as an embeddable Rust crate set (ann-*) plus the annopt CLI, not a long-running service. The library returns rich diagnostics (GraphDiagnostics, TuneResult) + optional trace hooks and surfaces failures as typed Result; it does not own a metrics registry, log sink, or HTTP endpoint. The integrating host (project 45 Vector Database, a search service, the ann-benchmarks harness) wires hooks into its own Prometheus/OTLP/log stack. Service concerns (scrape endpoint, alerting, on-call) live in the host; this section specifies the contract + a reference dashboard/SLO/runbook set.

26.1 Metrics a host should expose

Sourced from GraphDiagnostics, DistanceCounter, RecallHarness, Autotuner/TuneResult, DeleteSet. Sampled off the hot path (diagnostics are O(N·R)); never labeled by VectorId.

Metric Source Type Target / alert
annopt_build_seconds HnswBuilder/VamanaBuilder wall clock gauge per-corpus baseline ±20%
annopt_recall_at_k GraphDiagnostics.estimated_recall_at_10 (vs exact, sampled) gauge ≥ RecallTarget.recall (e.g. 0.95)
annopt_dist_comps_mean DistanceCounter / queries gauge ≤ CostModel prediction ×1.15
annopt_dist_comps_p99 per-query counter snapshot histogram ≤ 3× mean
annopt_search_latency_us{q=p50,p95,p99} host timer around greedy_search/beam_search histogram p50/p95/p99 ≤ SLO (§26.5)
annopt_mean_out_degree GraphDiagnostics.mean_out_degree gauge ∈ [0.6R, R]
annopt_orphan_count GraphDiagnostics.orphan_count gauge 0 post-optimize
annopt_connected_components GraphDiagnostics.connected_components gauge 1 post-optimize
annopt_unreachable_from_ep GraphDiagnostics.unreachable_from_ep gauge 0 post-optimize
annopt_navigability_score sampled expected-hops proxy gauge within ±10% of stamped baseline
annopt_tombstone_ratio DeleteSet popcount / VectorStore.len gauge > 0.20 → consolidate
annopt_pq_recall_delta recall(full) − recall(ProductQuantizer+rerank) via RecallHarness gauge < 0.03 recall points

Derived by host from raw counters: recall (offline, sampled vs exact KNN — never per-query), QPS, dist-comps/query, tombstone-filter rate. Cardinality: scalar per index instance; keep label sets (q, reject kind) < 20 series.

26.2 Trace hooks (optional, off by default)

SearchContext and the optimizer expose opt-in trace callbacks behind the trace feature — monomorphized no-op when disabled (zero branch in the SIMD inner loop). Answers "why did this query converge where it did?".

pub trait SearchTrace {
    fn on_expand(&mut self, node: VectorId, dist: Distance, hop: u32);
    fn on_frontier(&mut self, worst_in_w: Distance, w_len: u32);     // candidate frontier of size ef
    fn on_converge(&mut self, hops: u32, dist_comps: u64, results: &[SearchResult]);
}
pub trait OptimizeTrace {
    fn on_prune(&mut self, p: VectorId, kept: u32, dropped: u32, strat: PruneStrategy);
    fn on_repair(&mut self, added_reverse_edges: u32, reconnected_components: u32);
}

Captured per traced query: hop count, candidate frontier evolution (worst-in-W over hops), distance-comps, terminal stop reason (closest-unexpanded ≥ worst-in-W). Optimize trace: per-pass edges kept/dropped (occlusion_prune/degree_rebalance), reverse edges added (reverse_edge_repair), components reconnected (connect_components). Traces are returned to the host; ann-* never writes them anywhere. Sampling: head (e.g. 1/1000 searches) or tail (slow > p99); build/optimize/consolidate are low-frequency → always traceable.

26.3 Logging philosophy

  • The library does not log: no log!/tracing::event!, no stdout/stderr, no global state. A library cannot know the host's format/level/sink, and a hot path running millions of times/sec must not log.
  • Errors surface as typed Result<_, AnnError> with context: AnnError { Dim{expected,got}, NaNInput, DegreeExceeded{cap:R}, EfExceeded, CorruptGraph{offset,reason}, FormatVersion{file,supported}, Io(..) } — enough to act without a log line.
  • Degraded states surface as GraphDiagnostics fields (orphans, components, recall).
  • annopt (the CLI) is the only component that logs — it is the host in that context: owns tracing_subscriber, honors RUST_LOG, progress to stderr, results to stdout.

26.4 Host dashboard design

+----------------------------- annopt index: search ------------------------+
| recall@k (sampled) | p50/p95/p99 latency | dist-comps/query mean & p99    |
| [gauge, SLO 0.95]  | [3 lines vs bands]  | [line; rise => ef/degree drift]|
+----------------------------- topology / Pareto ----------------------------+
| recall vs p99 latency (Pareto: TuneResult points; current op-point pinned) |
| out-degree histogram (out_degree_hist[0..64]; spike at R => over-dense)     |
+----------------------------- connectivity / writes ------------------------+
| connected_components over time | orphan_count | tombstone_ratio (line@0.20) |
| (flatline at 1; step up = delete-induced split)                            |
+---------------------------------------------------------------------------+

The recall-vs-latency panel doubles as the operating-point selector: each point is a TuneResult { params, recall, mean_dist_comps, p99_us } from the Pareto frontier; host pins one. Top rows = SLOs; lower rows = leading indicators that move before the SLOs break.

26.5 SLO-style targets

SLO Target Window Error budget
Recall@10 (sampled offline vs exact KNN) ≥ RecallTarget.recall (e.g. 0.95) per index version regress > 0.01 → page; > 0.02 → block deploy
Search p50 / p95 / p99 ≤ host budget (e.g. 0.3 / 1 / 3 ms @ SIFT1M, R=64, tuned ef) rolling 5m p99 breach 5m → alert
Correctness zero panics on build/search/optimize/load (fuzz-gated, Phase 13) per build any panic → block
Connectivity 1 component, 0 orphans, 0 unreachable-from-EP post-optimize per optimize violation → block serve
Optimizer guarantee recall@k at fixed ef never decreases across an optimize pass per pass regression → drop pass, gate fixture
Build time ≤ corpus baseline per build > 1.2× → regression gate fail

Recall is a quality SLO measured offline on a held-out query set with RecallHarness exact ground truth, refreshed each build — not a live availability metric.

26.6 Incident response + runbooks

First action always: capture annopt diagnose output — GraphDiagnostics is the index's vitals (degree histogram, components, reachability-from-EP, orphans, tombstone count, metric/dim).

Symptom Likely cause Runbook
Recall dropped delete churn, stale graph, over-prune, under-set ef annopt diagnose old vs new → diff GraphDiagnostics; ef too low → tune to smallest ef meeting RecallTarget; over-pruned (mean_out_degree ≪ R) → relax alpha→1.0 / raise R, re-run occlusion_prune; orphans → connect_components + reverse_edge_repair; regressing pass → drop it, add PassReport delta as gate fixture; re-stamp
Search slow (p99↑, recall flat) ef too high, degree bloat, dist-comps↑, scalar fallback check mean_dist_comps vs CostModel; degree > R → degree_rebalance + occlusion_prune; ef drifted → re-tune; SIMD fell to scalar → rebuild with target-feature (AVX2/AVX-512/NEON), verify dispatch
Results missing orphan nodes, over-eager tombstones, split component diagnose: orphan_count/unreachable_from_ep/connected_components; reachable-but-absent → connect_components + reselect_entry_point; tombstoned-by-mistake → audit DeleteSet, rebuild from VectorStore
open/load fails or crash-loop FormatVersion skew, CorruptGraph (torn write), truncated mmap pin matching crate version or migrate via format-upgrade path; torn write → roll back to last clean stamped artifact; truncation → restore backup; never serve a partially-mapped CsrGraph
PQ recall delta high too few subspaces, no rerank raise ProductQuantizer.m_subspaces; enable DiskANN beam-search + full-precision re-rank

All runbooks operate on a copy, validate against the optimizer guarantee, then atomic-swap (§26.8) — never mutate a serving graph.

26.7 Deployment as a crate

  • SemVer: public Core Types are the API contract. On-disk CsrGraph carries a format_version: u16 header byte; mmap_graph rejects mismatches with AnnError::FormatVersion. A breaking change to ann-core/ann-graph layout = major bump + format-version increment; recall-preserving heuristic tweaks = minor; bench-only = patch. MSRV 1.78.
  • Feature flags (Cargo): simd (AVX2/AVX-512/NEON dispatch; off ⇒ portable scalar), pq (ProductQuantizer + on-disk compressed path), hnswlib-diff (test-only differential harness), trace (§26.2 hooks). Default ["simd"]; all features additive, none unify into unsafe-by-default.
  • No I/O in the search path: ann-core/ann-graph/ann-search are alloc-only; persistence isolated in ann-store. VisitedSet reuse → 0 bytes/query steady-state alloc (bench gate). Host controls when/where bytes hit disk.

26.8 Optimized-graph artifact lifecycle

build ----> optimize -----------------> stamp -------> serve -----> re-optimize
HnswBuilder occlusion_prune              header:        mmap_graph   (periodic /
VamanaBuilder degree_rebalance           {format_ver,   -> CsrGraph  tombstone-
(mutable    reverse_edge_repair          BuildParams,   (immutable,  triggered)
 Graph)     connect_components           diagnostics,   multi-reader      |
            reselect_entry_point         recall, ef,    lock-free)        |
                 |                        git_sha, ts}        ^   atomic   |
                 v  validate                                  |   rename   |
            GraphDiagnostics --- save_graph(CsrGraph,store) --+   swap -----+
            (gate: 1 comp, 0 orphan, recall >= target)
  • Stamp: serialized header embeds BuildParams, validated GraphDiagnostics, chosen SearchParams/TuneResult, format version, build timestamp + git SHA → reproducibility + audit.
  • Serve: mmap_graph → immutable CsrGraph; many concurrent readers, no locks (read-only); page-cache friendly via CSR contiguity. Hot index (CSR adjacency + PQ codes) should stay RAM-resident; with pq, full vectors may live on SSD reached via beam-batched reads.
  • Re-optimize cadence: trigger on tombstone_ratio > 0.20, connected_components > 1, or recall drift > 0.01. Runs on a copy; gate on the optimizer guarantee (recall non-regression at fixed ef); on pass, save_graph + atomic file rename + host reloads mmap. Failed gate → keep old artifact, alert.
  • Rollback: previous stamped artifact retained; swap is a rename, revert is a rename. Stamp metadata confirms which build is live. The in-memory Graph is the runtime source of truth — rebuild from VectorStore if no clean stamped artifact exists.

Compatibility Guarantees

Scope: stability contract across three independent axes — (1) source/API semver over the seven ann-* crates, (2) the versioned/checksummed on-disk graph + PQ binary, (3) recall reproducibility. Single-node; no wire protocol. Binary is annopt.

Semver & API Stability (ann-* crates)

  • Workspace versioned as one unit (lockstep). Post-1.0: MAJOR=breaking, MINOR=additive, PATCH=fix-only. Pre-1.0 0.y.z: y=may-break, z=additive/fix.
  • Primary stable surfaces (break only on MAJOR):
    • DistanceKernel { fn distance(&self, a: &[f32], b: &[f32]) -> Distance } — metric plug.
    • Element-of-search surface: greedy_search/beam_search signatures, SearchContext, SearchParams { k, ef, beam_width }, SearchResult { id, distance }.
    • Core newtypes/enums: VectorId(u32), Distance(f32), Metric, GraphKind, CsrGraph.
Tier Items Promise
Stable DistanceKernel, greedy_search/beam_search, Metric, Distance, VectorId, SearchParams, SearchResult, Graph/CsrGraph public fns, ProductQuantizer break = MAJOR
Evolving GraphDiagnostics fields, TuneResult, RecallTarget, PruneStrategy variants, GraphKind variants, DataType additive only; gated by #[non_exhaustive]
Internal VisitedSet bitset layout, candidate/result heap internals, SIMD kernel dispatch, AdjacencyList build-form layout, DistanceCounter no guarantee; may change in PATCH
